"""The optional Haiku classifier, driven entirely against a fake ``claude``.

No test here ever spawns the real binary: ``shutil.which`` and ``subprocess.run`` are both
monkeypatched, so the suite costs nothing, needs no login, and runs the same on a machine with no
Claude Code installed. What is proven is the contract around the call: the command line, the
scrubbed environment, the prompt travelling on stdin, and the fact that every failure shape returns
``None`` so the router can fall back to the deterministic scorer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
from typing import Any

import pytest

from lib import haiku, rules as rules_module

SITREP_ANSWER = {"skin": "sitrep", "confidence": 0.83, "reason": "status ask"}
# why: what the real CLI returns with --output-format json: the model's text under "result"
ENVELOPE = json.dumps({"result": json.dumps(SITREP_ANSWER)})
FAKE_CLAUDE = "C:/fake/bin/claude.cmd"  # why: the Windows spelling shutil.which really resolves to


class Recorder:
    """A stand-in for ``subprocess.run`` that records the call and returns a canned result."""

    def __init__(self, stdout: str = ENVELOPE, returncode: int = 0) -> None:
        self.stdout = stdout
        self.returncode = returncode
        self.calls: list[tuple[tuple[Any, ...], dict[str, Any]]] = []

    def __call__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
        self.calls.append((args, kwargs))
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
            args=args[0] if args else [], returncode=self.returncode, stdout=self.stdout, stderr=""
        )

    @property
    def argv(self) -> list[str]:
        return list(self.calls[0][0][0])

    @property
    def kwargs(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.calls[0][1]


@pytest.fixture
def rules() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The shipped algorithm, validated against the shipped skin files."""
    return rules_module.load_rules()


@pytest.fixture
def found_claude(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    """``claude`` is on PATH, at a Windows-shaped path."""
    monkeypatch.setattr("shutil.which", lambda name: FAKE_CLAUDE)


def _install(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, recorder: Recorder) -> Recorder:
    monkeypatch.setattr("subprocess.run", recorder)
    return recorder


def test_a_canned_envelope_becomes_a_haiku_decision(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())
    decision = haiku.classify("where does the members area stand", "", rules)
    assert decision is not None
    assert decision.skin == "sitrep"
    assert decision.tier == "E3"
    assert decision.source == "haiku"
    assert decision.score == 0.83
    assert decision.signals_top3 == [("haiku", 0.83)]
    assert decision.tie_with is None


def test_a_fenced_result_parses_too(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    fenced = "```json\n" + json.dumps(SITREP_ANSWER) + "\n```"
    _install(monkeypatch, Recorder(stdout=json.dumps({"result": fenced})))
    decision = haiku.classify("how is the signup flow going", "", rules)
    assert decision is not None
    assert decision.skin == "sitrep"
    assert decision.score == 0.83


def test_an_unknown_skin_is_refused(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    answer = json.dumps({"skin": "haiku-made-this-up", "confidence": 0.99, "reason": "no"})
    _install(monkeypatch, Recorder(stdout=json.dumps({"result": answer})))
    assert haiku.classify("anything", "", rules) is None


def test_unreadable_output_is_refused(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    _install(monkeypatch, Recorder(stdout="not json at all"))
    assert haiku.classify("anything", "", rules) is None


def test_a_non_zero_exit_is_refused(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    _install(monkeypatch, Recorder(returncode=1))
    assert haiku.classify("anything", "", rules) is None


def test_a_timeout_falls_back_to_the_rules(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    def timeout(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
        raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=FAKE_CLAUDE, timeout=haiku.CLASSIFIER_TIMEOUT_S)

    monkeypatch.setattr("subprocess.run", timeout)
    assert haiku.classify("where are we", "", rules) is None


def test_no_claude_on_path_is_refused(
    rules: dict[str, Any], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr("shutil.which", lambda name: None)
    recorder = _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())
    assert haiku.classify("where are we", "", rules) is None
    assert recorder.calls == []


def test_a_child_process_never_spawns_a_second_one(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_ROUTER_CHILD", "1")
    recorder = _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())
    assert haiku.classify("where are we", "", rules) is None
    assert recorder.calls == []


def test_the_child_environment_is_scrubbed_and_marked(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("CLAUDECODE", "1")
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "not-a-real-key")
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN", "not-a-real-token")
    recorder = _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())

    assert haiku.classify("where are we", "", rules) is not None
    env = recorder.kwargs["env"]
    assert "CLAUDECODE" not in env
    assert "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" not in env
    assert "ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN" not in env
    assert env["PROMPT_ROUTER_CHILD"] == "1"


def test_the_command_line_is_the_headless_one(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    recorder = _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())
    assert haiku.classify("where are we", "", rules) is not None

    argv = recorder.argv
    assert argv[0] == FAKE_CLAUDE  # the resolved path, so Windows runs claude.cmd without a shell
    assert "--print" in argv
    for flag, value in (
        ("--model", "haiku"),
        ("--output-format", "json"),
        ("--tools", ""),
        ("--max-turns", "1"),
        ("--setting-sources", "user"),
    ):
        assert argv[argv.index(flag) + 1] == value
    assert argv[argv.index("--system-prompt") + 1] == haiku.classifier_prompt(rules)
    assert recorder.kwargs["shell"] is False
    assert recorder.kwargs["timeout"] == haiku.CLASSIFIER_TIMEOUT_S
    assert "--bare" not in argv  # --bare never reads the member's login, so a subscription fails


def test_the_prompt_travels_on_stdin(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    recorder = _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())
    prompt = "where does the members area stand"
    assert haiku.classify(prompt, "", rules) is not None

    assert recorder.kwargs["input"] == prompt
    assert prompt not in recorder.argv


def test_recent_context_rides_in_front_of_the_prompt(
    rules: dict[str, Any], found_claude: None, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    recorder = _install(monkeypatch, Recorder())
    assert haiku.classify("and now", "we agreed the contract", rules) is not None
    assert recorder.kwargs["input"] == "we agreed the contract\n\nand now"


def test_the_classifier_prompt_names_every_skin_with_its_use_line(
    rules: dict[str, Any]
) -> None:
    text = haiku.classifier_prompt(rules)
    for name in rules["skins"]:
        assert f"- {name}: " in text
    assert len(rules["skins"]) == 8
    assert '"confidence": 0.0-1.0' in text
    assert text.count(" -> ") == len(haiku.EXAMPLES) == 8


def test_the_examples_are_not_the_demo_prompts(rules: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    demo = {
        "should i post the graph reel on tiktok too or keep it on instagram",
        "where are we on the members area",
        "go",
        "where were we",
        "what time is it /e5",
        "receipt",
        "oh fuck you claude",
    }
    used = {prompt.lower() for prompt, _, _, _ in haiku.EXAMPLES}
    assert used.isdisjoint(demo)
    assert {skin for _, skin, _, _ in haiku.EXAMPLES} == set(rules["skins"])



================================================
FILE: tests/test_hook_router.py
================================================
"""The router hook driven the way Claude Code drives it: a real process, JSON on stdin.

The assertions are about what reaches the harness: the router line at the top of the injected
context, exit 0 every time, and nothing at all on stdout whenever the router should stay out of the
way. The six routed demo prompts are here as they are typed in the walk, each with the margin it has
to clear over the runner-up, so a weight change that would blur one of them turns this file red.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from types import ModuleType
from typing import Any, Callable

import pytest

WS_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]  # why: tests/ sits directly under the workspace root
HOOKS_DIR = WS_ROOT / ".claude" / "hooks"  # why: the hook is launched by path, exactly as settings.json does
ROUTER_PATH = HOOKS_DIR / "router.py"
SKINS_LIB = HOOKS_DIR / "lib" / "skins.py"
SPARKLINE_LIB = HOOKS_DIR / "lib" / "sparkline.py"
SKINS_DIR = WS_ROOT / "skins"
HOOK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 60  # why: a hook that hangs must fail the test, never the whole run
WINDOWS_CWD = "C:\\Users\\x"  # why: a Windows cwd is the payload field that carries backslashes
MARGIN_FLOOR = 0.15  # why: the demo law, the winner sits this far clear of the runner-up so no frame is luck
MAX_BYTES = 7_500  # why: hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES, restated so this test measures the wire, not the constant
LEDGER_ROWS = 7  # why: the tallest contract the frames hold, and the widest injection the budget must fit

# why: the router line is the contract between the hook and every test; `source` can carry a space
ROUTER_LINE = re.compile(
    r"^\[router\] skin=(?P<skin>\S+) score=(?P<score>\S+) tier=(?P<tier>\S+) source=(?P<source>.+)$"
)

# why: the walk's own prompts, typed exactly as the frames spell them
FRAME_1 = "should I post the graph reel on tiktok too or keep it on instagram"
FRAME_2 = "where are we on the members area"
FRAME_3 = (
    "ok the members area needs to work on mobile, email-only login, $47 everywhere no $29 left, "
    "don't touch checkout, skool opens a new tab, feels premium, due friday"
)
FRAME_6 = "what time is it /e5"
FRAME_7 = "receipt"
FRAME_8 = "oh fuck you claude"

# why: the four frames the algorithm scores; the margin law applies to these and to no typed token
SCORED_FRAMES = [
    (FRAME_1, "bluf", "E2"),
    (FRAME_2, "sitrep", "E3"),
    (FRAME_3, "contract", "E4"),
    (FRAME_8, "banter", "E1"),
]

# why: the two the member typed outright; a typed token is a certainty, so it is scored 1.00
TYPED_FRAMES = [
    (FRAME_6, "contract", "E5"),
    (FRAME_7, "receipt", "E2"),
]

# why: the router renders through lib/skins.py and the eight skin files;
# without them there is nothing to drive, so these tests say so rather than fail as a red.
_MISSING = [str(path) for path in (SKINS_LIB, SPARKLINE_LIB) if not path.is_file()]
if not SKINS_DIR.is_dir() or not list(SKINS_DIR.glob("*.md")):
    _MISSING.append(str(SKINS_DIR))
pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(
    bool(_MISSING), reason="skin files missing: " + ", ".join(_MISSING)
)


def payload(prompt: str, session_id: str = "s1") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The UserPromptSubmit payload, the three fields the router reads."""
    return {"session_id": session_id, "prompt": prompt, "cwd": WINDOWS_CWD}


def context(stdout: str) -> str:
    """The additional context out of the one JSON object the hook wrote."""
    envelope = json.loads(stdout)
    assert envelope["hookSpecificOutput"]["hookEventName"] == "UserPromptSubmit"
    return envelope["hookSpecificOutput"]["additionalContext"]


def router_fields(injected: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """The router line read back as its four fields."""
    match = ROUTER_LINE.match(injected.split("\n")[0])
    assert match is not None, f"no router line at the top of: {injected[:120]!r}"
    return match.groupdict()


def test_trailing_token_routes_to_sitrep(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("anything /sitrep"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert done.stderr == ""
    assert context(done.stdout).startswith(
        "[router] skin=sitrep score=1.00 tier=E3 source=explicit"
    )


def test_a_windows_payload_with_lone_backslashes_still_parses() -> None:
    # why: Claude Code on Windows can send this exact spelling, which is not valid JSON
    raw = '{"session_id": "s1", "prompt": "anything /sitrep", "cwd": "C:\\Users\\x"}'
    assert "\\\\" not in raw

    done = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(ROUTER_PATH)],
        input=raw.encode("utf-8"),
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=HOOK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
        check=False,
    )

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert context(done.stdout.decode("utf-8")).startswith("[router] skin=sitrep")


def test_an_empty_prompt_says_nothing(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("   "))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert done.stdout == ""


def test_a_bare_slash_command_says_nothing(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("/config"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert done.stdout == ""


def test_a_tier_token_on_its_own_opens_a_contract(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("/e5"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert context(done.stdout).startswith(
        "[router] skin=contract score=1.00 tier=E5 source=explicit"
    )


def test_a_plain_prompt_falls_back_to_bluf(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("what is the capital of peru"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert context(done.stdout).startswith("[router] skin=bluf score=0.00 tier=E2 source=default")


def test_a_pasted_log_routes_and_stays_inside_the_budget(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    # why: a member pastes a stack trace or a build log; the injection must still fit the cap
    pasted = "\n".join("2026-08-18 20:14:07 build line " + str(number) for number in range(1200))
    assert len(pasted.encode("utf-8")) > 30_000

    done = run_hook("router", payload(pasted))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert isinstance(json.loads(done.stdout), dict)
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert injected.startswith("[router] skin=")
    assert len(injected.encode("utf-8")) <= MAX_BYTES


def test_a_non_ascii_prompt_survives_the_wire(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("qué tal el café ☕ /sitrep"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert context(done.stdout).startswith("[router] skin=sitrep")


def test_a_non_ascii_prompt_with_no_token_still_carries_the_router_line(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any],
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("¿dónde estamos con el café ☕ y los emojis 🙂?"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["skin"]


def test_a_child_process_stands_down(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("anything /sitrep"), env={"PROMPT_ROUTER_CHILD": "1"})

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert done.stdout == ""


def test_a_render_failure_stays_silent_and_is_logged(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    state_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    from lib import skins

    def fall_over(*args: object, **kwargs: object) -> str:
        raise RuntimeError("render fell over")

    monkeypatch.setattr(skins, "render_injection", fall_over)

    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(router_hook, payload("anything /sitrep"), capsys, monkeypatch)

    assert out == ""
    assert code == 0
    logged = (state_dir / "hooks.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "Traceback" in logged
    assert "render fell over" in logged


def write_house(state_dir: Path, skin: str) -> None:
    """Seed the member's standing floor, the way the `default <skin>` command writes it."""
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "house.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"skin": skin}), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n"
    )


def test_a_house_floor_replaces_the_default(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    write_house(state_dir, "sitrep")

    done = run_hook("router", payload("what is the capital of peru"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert context(done.stdout).startswith("[router] skin=sitrep score=0.00 tier=E3 source=house")


def test_a_house_floor_set_to_off_leaves_the_default(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    write_house(state_dir, "off")

    done = run_hook("router", payload("what is the capital of peru"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert context(done.stdout).startswith("[router] skin=bluf score=0.00 tier=E2 source=default")


def test_a_ledger_on_disk_reaches_the_injection(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "counter").write_text("41", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    (state_dir / "ledger.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "contract_id": "0042",
                "title": "members area",
                "approved_at": "2026-08-18T20:10:00",
                "rows": [
                    {
                        "id": "R1",
                        "status": "●",
                        "text": "works on mobile",
                        "proof": "screenshot 375px, no h-scroll",
                    }
                ],
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
        newline="\n",
    )

    done = run_hook("router", payload("anything /sitrep"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert " R1 ● works on mobile · proof: screenshot 375px, no h-scroll" in injected
    assert "0042" in injected


def test_every_run_writes_one_telemetry_line(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    run_hook("router", payload("anything /sitrep"))
    run_hook("router", payload("what is the capital of peru"))
    # why: two panes on one repo share the state folder by design, and both must be recorded
    run_hook("router", payload("go", session_id="s2"))

    lines = [
        line
        for line in (state_dir / "runs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]
    assert len(lines) == 3
    records = [json.loads(line) for line in lines]
    assert [record["session_id"] for record in records] == ["s1", "s1", "s2"]
    assert [record["skin"] for record in records] == ["sitrep", "bluf", "bars"]
    assert [record["event"] for record in records] == ["route", "route", "route"]


# --- the walk's own prompts, the house command, the classifier switch -------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("prompt", "skin", "tier"), SCORED_FRAMES)
def test_a_scored_demo_prompt_lands_on_its_skin(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], prompt: str, skin: str, tier: str
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload(prompt))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    fields = router_fields(context(done.stdout))
    assert fields["skin"] == skin
    assert fields["tier"] == tier
    assert fields["source"] == "rules"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("prompt", "skin", "tier"), TYPED_FRAMES)
def test_a_typed_demo_prompt_lands_on_its_skin(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], prompt: str, skin: str, tier: str
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload(prompt))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    fields = router_fields(context(done.stdout))
    assert fields["skin"] == skin
    assert fields["tier"] == tier
    assert fields["source"] == "explicit"
    assert fields["score"] == "1.00"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("prompt", "skin", "tier"), SCORED_FRAMES)
def test_a_scored_demo_prompt_clears_the_margin_law(prompt: str, skin: str, tier: str) -> None:
    from lib import score
    from lib.rules import load_rules
    from lib.signals import State

    rules = load_rules()

    assert score.decide(prompt, State(), rules).skin == skin
    assert score.margin(prompt, State(), rules) >= MARGIN_FLOOR


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("prompt", "skin", "tier"), TYPED_FRAMES)
def test_a_typed_demo_prompt_is_a_certainty(prompt: str, skin: str, tier: str) -> None:
    from lib import score
    from lib.rules import load_rules
    from lib.signals import State

    rules = load_rules()

    assert score.explicit_decision(prompt, rules) is not None
    assert score.margin(prompt, State(), rules) == 1.0


def test_the_house_command_sets_the_floor_and_the_next_plain_prompt_takes_it(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("default sitrep"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert "house skin set to sitrep" in context(done.stdout)
    assert json.loads((state_dir / "house.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) == {"skin": "sitrep"}

    after = run_hook("router", payload("what is the capital of peru"))

    assert context(after.stdout).startswith("[router] skin=sitrep score=0.00 tier=E3 source=house")


def test_the_house_command_is_recorded_and_never_renders_a_skin(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("default board"))

    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert injected.splitlines()[0] == "[router] house skin set to board"
    # why: a house turn is a confirmation, so no skin shape may reach the model on it
    assert "Render the" not in injected
    record = json.loads((state_dir / "runs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[0])
    assert record["skin"] == "board"
    assert record["source"] == "house"


def test_default_off_hands_the_floor_back(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    run_hook("router", payload("default sitrep"))

    done = run_hook("router", payload("default off"))

    assert "house skin set to off" in context(done.stdout)
    assert json.loads((state_dir / "house.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) == {"skin": "off"}

    after = run_hook("router", payload("what is the capital of peru"))

    assert context(after.stdout).startswith("[router] skin=bluf score=0.00 tier=E2 source=default")


def test_a_sentence_that_merely_mentions_the_command_is_not_the_command(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload("should we make default sitrep the house style or not"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert not (state_dir / "house.json").exists()
    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["source"] == "rules"


def write_ledger(state_dir: Path, rows: int = LEDGER_ROWS) -> None:
    """A full contract on disk: the widest ledger view the injection ever carries."""
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "ledger.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "contract_id": "0042",
                "title": "members area",
                "approved_at": "2026-08-18T20:10:00",
                "rows": [
                    {
                        "id": f"R{number}",
                        "status": "●" if number % 2 else "○",
                        "text": f"requirement {number} reads as one full line of member wording",
                        "proof": f"pytest tests/test_r{number}.py -q, one line of output",
                    }
                    for number in range(1, rows + 1)
                ],
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
        newline="\n",
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("prompt", [FRAME_2, FRAME_3, FRAME_7, "anything /bluf"])
def test_a_seven_row_ledger_still_fits_the_budget(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path, prompt: str
) -> None:
    write_ledger(state_dir)

    done = run_hook("router", payload(prompt))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert len(injected.encode("utf-8")) <= MAX_BYTES
    assert injected.startswith("[router] skin=")


def test_the_seven_ledger_rows_reach_the_injection(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    write_ledger(state_dir)

    done = run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_2))

    injected = context(done.stdout)
    for number in range(1, LEDGER_ROWS + 1):
        assert f" R{number} " in injected


def haiku_rules(tmp_path: Path, ws_root: Path) -> Path:
    """The shipped rules with the classifier switched on, written to this test's own file."""
    data = json.loads((ws_root / "algorithm" / "rules.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    data["classifier"] = "haiku"
    path = tmp_path / "rules-haiku.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    return path


def test_the_classifier_answers_when_the_rules_switch_it_on(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    ws_root: Path,
) -> None:
    from lib import haiku
    from lib import rules as rules_lib
    from lib.score import Decision

    real_load = rules_lib.load_rules
    switched_on = haiku_rules(tmp_path, ws_root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rules_lib, "load_rules", lambda path=None: real_load(switched_on))

    seen: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def answer(prompt: str, recent_context: str, rules: dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        seen["prompt"] = prompt
        seen["context"] = recent_context
        seen["classifier"] = rules.get("classifier")
        return Decision(
            skin="receipt", tier="E2", score=0.91, source="haiku", signals_top3=[("haiku", 0.91)]
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(haiku, "classify", answer)

    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(
        router_hook, payload("so what did you actually change in there"), capsys, monkeypatch
    )

    assert code == 0
    assert seen["prompt"] == "so what did you actually change in there"
    assert seen["context"] == ""
    assert seen["classifier"] == "haiku"
    assert context(out).startswith("[router] skin=receipt score=0.91 tier=E2 source=haiku")


def test_a_classifier_that_cannot_answer_hands_the_turn_to_the_rules(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    ws_root: Path,
) -> None:
    from lib import haiku
    from lib import rules as rules_lib

    real_load = rules_lib.load_rules
    switched_on = haiku_rules(tmp_path, ws_root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rules_lib, "load_rules", lambda path=None: real_load(switched_on))
    monkeypatch.setattr(haiku, "classify", lambda *args, **kwargs: None)

    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(router_hook, payload(FRAME_2), capsys, monkeypatch)

    assert code == 0
    fields = router_fields(context(out))
    assert fields["skin"] == "sitrep"
    assert fields["source"] == "rules (haiku failed)"


def test_a_classifier_that_cannot_answer_keeps_the_real_source_on_the_line(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    ws_root: Path,
) -> None:
    """When nothing scores after a failed classifier, the line still says the default answered."""
    from lib import haiku
    from lib import rules as rules_lib

    real_load = rules_lib.load_rules
    switched_on = haiku_rules(tmp_path, ws_root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rules_lib, "load_rules", lambda path=None: real_load(switched_on))
    monkeypatch.setattr(haiku, "classify", lambda *args, **kwargs: None)

    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(
        router_hook, payload("what is the capital of peru"), capsys, monkeypatch
    )

    assert code == 0
    fields = router_fields(context(out))
    assert fields["skin"] == "bluf"
    assert fields["source"] == "default (haiku failed)"


def test_an_explicit_token_never_reaches_the_classifier(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    ws_root: Path,
) -> None:
    from lib import haiku
    from lib import rules as rules_lib

    real_load = rules_lib.load_rules
    switched_on = haiku_rules(tmp_path, ws_root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rules_lib, "load_rules", lambda path=None: real_load(switched_on))
    calls: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(haiku, "classify", lambda *args, **kwargs: calls.append(args))

    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(router_hook, payload(FRAME_6), capsys, monkeypatch)

    assert code == 0
    assert calls == []
    assert router_fields(context(out))["source"] == "explicit"


def test_the_shipped_rules_never_spend_a_model_call(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:

    assert calls == []
    assert router_fields(context(out))["skin"] == "sitrep"


# --- the contract turns (go, a numbered edit, the catch-up after a /clear) ---------------------

CONTRACT_ID = "0042"  # why: the number the frames print, so these turns read as the walk shows them
CONTRACT_TITLE = "members area"

# why: three rows of the frame-3 contract: one to reword, one to strike, one deadline
CONTRACT_ROWS: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = [
    ("R3", "$47 everywhere", "grep -rn 29 src", ""),
    ("R6", "feels premium", "python -c \"print(1)\"", ""),
    ("R7", "ship it by friday", "T-2d", ""),
]

FRAME_4 = "go"  # why: the word that agrees the contract and starts the build
FRAME_5 = "where were we"  # why: the first thing typed into a window that has just been cleared
STRIKE = "strike R6"
REWORD = "R3: $47 everywhere, no $29 anywhere"
SAY_GO = "say go · strike or reword any line by number"  # why: the closing line of an edit turn
LITE_SLOT = "<one line: what is still theirs, from the open rows>"
SLEEPY_CAT = "( -.- )"  # why: the middle row of the sleepy cat, the one that reads as just woken


def build_contract() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """An unapproved contract on disk, written through the ledger rather than by hand."""
    from lib import ledger
    from lib.rules import load_rules

    prefixes = load_rules()["proofs"]["allow_prefixes"]
    written = ledger.from_table_rows(
        CONTRACT_ROWS, CONTRACT_TITLE, "s1", prefixes, contract_id=CONTRACT_ID
    )
    assert written["approved_at"] is None
    return written


def read_ledger() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The contract as it stands on disk after a hook has run."""
    from lib import ledger

    written = ledger.load()
    assert written is not None
    return written


def row_of(written: dict[str, Any], row_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One row of the contract by its number."""
    found = [row for row in written["rows"] if row["id"] == row_id]
    assert found, f"{row_id} is not on the contract"
    return found[0]


def test_go_agrees_the_contract_and_lands_on_bars(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_4))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["skin"] == "bars"
    assert read_ledger()["approved_at"]


def test_go_said_twice_agrees_it_once(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    build_contract()

    run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_4))
    agreed_at = read_ledger()["approved_at"]
    done = run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_4))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["skin"] == "bars"
    # why: go is said once; a second go must not restart the clock on a build already running
    assert read_ledger()["approved_at"] == agreed_at


def test_go_with_no_contract_still_routes_to_bars(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_4))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["skin"] == "bars"


def test_a_struck_row_is_answered_with_the_table_again(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload(STRIKE))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    fields = router_fields(injected)
    assert fields["skin"] == "contract"
    assert fields["source"] == "edit"
    assert fields["score"] == "1.00"
    assert injected.splitlines()[1] == "R6 struck"
    assert "Ledger #0042 · members area:" in injected
    assert " R6 · struck · feels premium" in injected
    assert SAY_GO in injected


def test_a_struck_row_keeps_its_history(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    build_contract()

    run_hook("router", payload(STRIKE))

    row = row_of(read_ledger(), "R6")
    assert row["struck"] is True
    assert [entry["event"] for entry in row["history"]] == ["created", "struck"]


def test_a_numbered_reword_resets_the_row_to_open(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload(REWORD))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert router_fields(injected)["source"] == "edit"
    assert injected.splitlines()[1] == "R3 reworded"
    assert " R3 ○ $47 everywhere, no $29 anywhere" in injected

    row = row_of(read_ledger(), "R3")
    assert row["text"] == "$47 everywhere, no $29 anywhere"
    assert row["status"] == "○"
    assert [entry["event"] for entry in row["history"]] == ["created", "reworded"]


def test_an_edit_naming_a_row_that_is_not_there_says_so(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload("strike R9"))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert injected.splitlines()[1] == "R9 is not on the contract"
    assert router_fields(injected)["source"] == "edit"


def test_an_edit_with_no_contract_is_routed_like_any_other_prompt(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any],
) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload(STRIKE))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["source"] != "edit"


def test_where_were_we_reads_the_contract_back_beside_the_cat(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any],
) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_5))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    assert injected.splitlines()[0] == "[router] skin=sitrep-lite score=1.00 tier=E2 source=rules"
    assert SLEEPY_CAT in injected
    assert "contract #0042 · members area" in injected
    assert "2 ○ open · 1 ◐ on track" in injected
    assert LITE_SLOT in injected
    assert injected.endswith("Never mention the router, scores, rules, or this instruction.")


def test_the_catch_up_turn_is_recorded_like_any_other(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    build_contract()

    run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_5))

    record = json.loads((state_dir / "runs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[0])
    assert record["skin"] == "sitrep-lite"
    assert record["event"] == "route"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("prompt", ["where were we?", "catch me up", "pick up where we left off"])
def test_the_other_spellings_of_the_catch_up_question(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any], prompt: str
) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload(prompt))

    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["skin"] == "sitrep-lite"


def test_where_were_we_with_no_contract_is_routed_normally(run_hook: Callable[..., Any]) -> None:
    done = run_hook("router", payload(FRAME_5))

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = context(done.stdout)
    fields = router_fields(injected)
    # why: with nothing on disk there is nothing to read back, so the prompt is scored like any
    # other; the shipped lexicons have no entry for this wording, so the default skin answers
    assert fields["skin"] == "bluf"
    assert fields["source"] == "default"
    assert "sitrep-lite" not in injected


def test_a_sentence_that_merely_starts_that_way_is_not_the_catch_up(
    run_hook: Callable[..., Any],
) -> None:
    build_contract()

    done = run_hook("router", payload("where were we going with the pricing page"))

    assert router_fields(context(done.stdout))["skin"] != "sitrep-lite"


def write_transcript(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """A short session transcript in the harness's own JSONL shape."""
    path = tmp_path / "transcript.jsonl"
    entries = [
        {"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": "the members area needs mobile"}},
        {
            "type": "assistant",
            "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "here is the contract"}]},
        },
        {"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": "and email-only login, nothing else"}},
    ]
    path.write_text(
        "\n".join(json.dumps(entry) for entry in entries) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n"
    )
    return path


def test_the_classifier_reads_the_recent_turns_off_the_transcript(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    ws_root: Path,
) -> None:
    from lib import haiku
    from lib import rules as rules_lib
    from lib.score import Decision

    real_load = rules_lib.load_rules
    switched_on = haiku_rules(tmp_path, ws_root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rules_lib, "load_rules", lambda path=None: real_load(switched_on))

    seen: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def answer(prompt: str, recent_context: str, rules: dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        seen["prompt"] = prompt
        seen["context"] = recent_context
        return Decision(skin="sitrep", tier="E3", score=0.8, source="haiku")

    monkeypatch.setattr(haiku, "classify", answer)

    turn = payload("and the price?")
    turn["transcript_path"] = str(write_transcript(tmp_path))
    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(router_hook, turn, capsys, monkeypatch)

    assert code == 0
    assert seen["prompt"] == "and the price?"
    # why: a three-word prompt only means something next to the turns before it
    assert "user: and email-only login, nothing else" in seen["context"]
    assert "assistant: here is the contract" in seen["context"]
    assert context(out).startswith("[router] skin=sitrep")


def test_a_transcript_path_that_is_not_there_costs_the_turn_nothing(
    router_hook: ModuleType,
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., tuple[str, int]],
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    ws_root: Path,
) -> None:
    from lib import haiku
    from lib import rules as rules_lib
    from lib.score import Decision

    real_load = rules_lib.load_rules
    switched_on = haiku_rules(tmp_path, ws_root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rules_lib, "load_rules", lambda path=None: real_load(switched_on))

    seen: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def answer(prompt: str, recent_context: str, rules: dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        seen["context"] = recent_context
        return Decision(skin="bluf", tier="E2", score=0.7, source="haiku")

    monkeypatch.setattr(haiku, "classify", answer)

    turn = payload("and the price?")
    turn["transcript_path"] = str(tmp_path / "gone.jsonl")
    out, code = call_hook_inprocess(router_hook, turn, capsys, monkeypatch)

    assert code == 0
    assert seen["context"] == ""
    assert context(out).startswith("[router] skin=bluf")



================================================
FILE: tests/test_hookio.py
================================================
"""Tests for lib/hookio.py: stdin repair, the stdout envelope, the byte clip, state and logging."""

from __future__ import annotations

import io
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

import pytest

from lib import hookio, ledger

# why: the exact bytes Claude Code expects back from a UserPromptSubmit hook.
CONTEXT_ENVELOPE = (
    '{"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "UserPromptSubmit", "additionalContext": "x"}}'
)

# why: the exact bytes the Stop gate writes when it holds the turn.
BLOCK_ENVELOPE = '{"decision": "block", "reason": "R3 unproven"}'

# why: a real Windows payload, spelled with single backslashes, which is invalid JSON.
WINDOWS_PAYLOAD = r'{"cwd": "C:\Users\x\proj", "prompt": "hi"}'

# why: accents, the separator the frames use and a tick, the three shapes a member's own words
# carry that a console codepage mangles.
ACCENTED = "café · ünïcode ✔"

# why: a hook run as a subprocess must never outlive the test that started it.
HOOK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 60


def _feed(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, raw: str) -> None:
    """Put ``raw`` on stdin for the next read_hook_input() call."""
    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.stdin", io.StringIO(raw))


def run_hook_utf8(hook_path: Path, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
    """Run a hook as a real subprocess with the payload on stdin as UTF-8 bytes.

    Claude Code's own encoder leaves non-ASCII alone, so a member's accents reach the hook as UTF-8
    bytes rather than as ``\\uXXXX`` escapes. The shared ``run_hook`` helper escapes to ASCII, which
    would hide the very thing this test is about, so the payload is spelled the way the harness
    spells it.
    """
    return subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(hook_path)],
        input=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=HOOK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
        check=False,
    )


# --- read_hook_input ---------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_windows_lone_backslash_payload_parses_via_the_repair(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(json.JSONDecodeError):
        json.loads(WINDOWS_PAYLOAD)  # the payload really is invalid JSON before the repair

    _feed(monkeypatch, WINDOWS_PAYLOAD)
    data = hookio.read_hook_input()

    assert data == {"cwd": r"C:\Users\x\proj", "prompt": "hi"}


def test_valid_json_parses_unchanged(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    payload = {"cwd": r"C:\Users\x\proj", "prompt": "where are we", "session_id": "abc"}
    _feed(monkeypatch, json.dumps(payload))

    assert hookio.read_hook_input() == payload


def test_empty_stdin_is_an_empty_dict(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    _feed(monkeypatch, "")

    assert hookio.read_hook_input() == {}


def test_unparseable_stdin_is_an_empty_dict_and_is_logged(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, state_dir: Path
) -> None:
    _feed(monkeypatch, "{not json at all")

    assert hookio.read_hook_input() == {}
    assert "did not parse" in (state_dir / "hooks.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_a_json_payload_that_is_not_an_object_is_an_empty_dict(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    _feed(monkeypatch, "[1, 2, 3]")

    assert hookio.read_hook_input() == {}


def test_a_text_stdin_with_no_buffer_is_still_read(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    """A stdin that is already text, as a StringIO is, needs no decoding and keeps its characters."""
    _feed(monkeypatch, json.dumps({"prompt": ACCENTED}, ensure_ascii=False))

    assert hookio.read_hook_input() == {"prompt": ACCENTED}


def test_a_prompt_with_accents_reaches_a_real_hook_intact(ws_root: Path, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """The characters a member types survive the trip through the real router process.

    The console codepage decides nothing here: the bytes are UTF-8 on the wire and UTF-8 when they
    are read. The reword is the member's own words landing in the contract, which is where the
    mangling showed up: "café" reached the ledger as "cafÃ©".
    """
    ledger.from_table_rows(
        [("R3", "feels premium", "undefined · pick a measurable", "")],
        "members area",
        "s1",
        [],
        contract_id="0042",
    )

    done = run_hook_utf8(
        ws_root / ".claude" / "hooks" / "router.py",
        {"session_id": "s1", "prompt": f"R3: {ACCENTED}", "cwd": str(tmp_path)},
    )

    assert done.returncode == 0
    injected = json.loads(done.stdout.decode("utf-8"))["hookSpecificOutput"]["additionalContext"]
    assert injected.startswith("[router] skin=contract")
    assert ACCENTED in injected
    loaded = ledger.load()
    assert loaded is not None
    assert loaded["rows"][0]["text"] == ACCENTED


# --- clip_bytes --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_clip_bytes_returns_text_unchanged_when_it_fits() -> None:
    text = "[router] skin=bluf\nShape:\n  line"

    assert hookio.clip_bytes(text, hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES) == text


def test_clip_bytes_drops_whole_lines_from_the_end() -> None:
    lines = ["┌───┐", "│ a │", "│ b │", "└───┘"]  # 5 box glyphs each, 3 bytes per glyph
    text = "\n".join(lines)
    budget = len("\n".join(lines[:2]).encode("utf-8"))

    out = hookio.clip_bytes(text, budget)

    assert out == "\n".join(lines[:2])
    assert len(out.encode("utf-8")) <= budget
    assert out.encode("utf-8").decode("utf-8") == out  # no split codepoint
    assert all(line in lines for line in out.split("\n"))  # every kept line is whole
    assert text.startswith(out)  # the clip ends on a line boundary


def test_clip_bytes_drops_a_line_it_cannot_fit_whole() -> None:
    # The budget lands in the middle of line two: a byte cut would keep half of it, and the
    # contract is whole lines, so line two goes entirely.
    text = "┌───┐\n│ a long row │\n└───┘"
    budget = len("┌───┐\n│ a long".encode("utf-8"))

    out = hookio.clip_bytes(text, budget)

    assert out == "┌───┐"
    assert out.encode("utf-8").decode("utf-8") == out


def test_clip_bytes_stops_at_the_first_line_that_does_not_fit() -> None:
    # A shorter line further down is never pulled up past a dropped one: the injection is
    # ordered top-first, so the clip is a prefix.
    text = "A\nBBBBBBBBBB\nC"

    out = hookio.clip_bytes(text, 5)

    assert out == "A"


def test_clip_bytes_always_keeps_the_first_line() -> None:
    lines = ["│ the router line │", "│ dropped │", "│ dropped │"]
    text = "\n".join(lines)

    out = hookio.clip_bytes(text, len(lines[0].encode("utf-8")))

    assert out == lines[0]


def test_clip_bytes_never_splits_a_codepoint_when_line_one_alone_exceeds() -> None:
    text = "█████\nsecond line"
    budget = 7  # each "█" is 3 bytes, so two fit and a third would reach 9

    out = hookio.clip_bytes(text, budget)

    assert out == "██"
    assert len(out.encode("utf-8")) <= budget
    assert out.encode("utf-8").decode("utf-8") == out


def test_clip_bytes_at_a_budget_smaller_than_one_glyph_returns_empty() -> None:
    assert hookio.clip_bytes("█ head\ntail", 2) == ""


def test_router_max_bytes_is_the_agreed_budget() -> None:
    assert hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES == 7_500


# --- emit_context / emit_block / emit_nothing ----------------------------------------------


def test_emit_context_is_byte_exact_with_no_trailing_newline(
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
) -> None:
    hookio.emit_context("UserPromptSubmit", "x")

    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert out == CONTEXT_ENVELOPE
    assert not out.endswith("\n")


def test_emit_context_escapes_non_ascii(capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    hookio.emit_context("UserPromptSubmit", "▁█")

    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "\\u2581" in out
    assert "\\u2588" in out
    assert "▁" not in out
    assert json.loads(out)["hookSpecificOutput"]["additionalContext"] == "▁█"


def test_emit_block_is_byte_exact(capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    hookio.emit_block("R3 unproven")

    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert out == BLOCK_ENVELOPE
    assert not out.endswith("\n")


def test_emit_nothing_writes_nothing(capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    hookio.emit_nothing()

    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    assert captured.out == ""
    assert captured.err == ""


# --- files: atomic_write_json / read_json ---------------------------------------------------


def test_atomic_write_json_writes_utf8_with_lf_only(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "ledger.json"

    hookio.atomic_write_json(target, {"title": "café ▁", "rows": [1, 2]})

    raw = target.read_bytes()
    assert b"\r" not in raw
    assert "café ▁" in raw.decode("utf-8")  # ensure_ascii=False on disk
    assert json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))["rows"] == [1, 2]


def test_atomic_write_json_leaves_no_temp_file_behind(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "house.json"

    hookio.atomic_write_json(target, {"skin": "bluf"})

    assert [p.name for p in tmp_path.iterdir()] == ["house.json"]


def test_atomic_write_json_replaces_an_existing_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "house.json"
    hookio.atomic_write_json(target, {"skin": "bluf"})

    hookio.atomic_write_json(target, {"skin": "sitrep"})

    assert hookio.read_json(target) == {"skin": "sitrep"}


def test_read_json_returns_the_default_when_the_file_is_missing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert hookio.read_json(tmp_path / "absent.json", {}) == {}
    assert hookio.read_json(tmp_path / "absent.json") is None


def test_read_json_returns_the_default_when_the_file_is_not_json(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    broken = tmp_path / "broken.json"
    broken.write_text("half a file {", encoding="utf-8")

    assert hookio.read_json(broken, {"fallback": True}) == {"fallback": True}


# --- state_dir / repo_root / is_child_process -----------------------------------------------


def test_the_lib_import_resolves_inside_this_workspace(ws_root: Path) -> None:
    assert Path(hookio.__file__).resolve().parent == ws_root / ".claude" / "hooks" / "lib"


def test_the_harness_helpers_are_exposed_as_fixtures(
    load_hook: Callable[..., object],
    run_hook: Callable[..., object],
    call_hook_inprocess: Callable[..., object],
) -> None:
    assert callable(load_hook)
    assert callable(run_hook)
    assert callable(call_hook_inprocess)


def test_repo_root_is_the_workspace_root(ws_root: Path) -> None:
    assert hookio.repo_root() == ws_root
    assert (hookio.repo_root() / ".claude" / "hooks" / "lib" / "hookio.py").is_file()


def test_state_dir_reads_the_env_at_call_time(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    first = tmp_path / "one"
    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_ROUTER_STATE_DIR", str(first))
    assert hookio.state_dir() == first
    assert first.is_dir()

    second = tmp_path / "two" / "nested"
    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_ROUTER_STATE_DIR", str(second))
    assert hookio.state_dir() == second
    assert second.is_dir()


def test_state_dir_falls_back_to_the_repo_router_folder(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.delenv("PROMPT_ROUTER_STATE_DIR", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, "mkdir", lambda *args, **kwargs: None)  # never touch the real repo

    assert hookio.state_dir() == hookio.repo_root() / ".router"


def test_is_child_process_reflects_the_env(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    assert hookio.is_child_process() is False

    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_ROUTER_CHILD", "1")
    assert hookio.is_child_process() is True

    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_ROUTER_CHILD", "")
    assert hookio.is_child_process() is False

def test_log_appends_when_the_dir_is_writable(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    hookio.log("router: routed bluf")
    hookio.log("gate: no contract table")

    lines = (state_dir / "hooks.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    assert len(lines) == 2
    assert lines[0].endswith("router: routed bluf")
    assert lines[1].endswith("gate: no contract table")


def test_log_writes_lf_only(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    hookio.log("router: routed sitrep")

    assert b"\r" not in (state_dir / "hooks.log").read_bytes()


def test_log_never_raises_when_the_dir_is_unwritable(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    def refuse(*args: object, **kwargs: object) -> None:
        raise OSError("read-only file system")

    monkeypatch.setattr("builtins.open", refuse)

    assert hookio.log("this must not raise") is None



================================================
FILE: tests/test_ledger.py

"""The contract ledger: numbering, kinds, edits, proofs, and what the injection reads back.

Two disciplines run through this file. Cold-start twins: every numbering test runs once on an
empty state folder and once on a seeded one, because #0001 and #0042 are different code paths for
the member. The status by operation grid: the surprising cells are asserted rather than assumed,
so a strike on a proven row, a reword on a failed one and a second ``go`` all have a test each.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

# harness fallback: tests/conftest.py puts <WS>/.claude/hooks on sys.path; this insert is a no-op
# once it has, and keeps `from lib import ledger` resolving when this file is run alone.
_HOOKS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / ".claude" / "hooks"
if str(_HOOKS_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HOOKS_DIR))

from lib import ledger  # noqa: E402

WS_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

# why: the shipped allowlist, not a hand-written copy: the kinds a member sees depend on this file
ALLOW_PREFIXES: list[str] = json.loads(
    (WS_ROOT / "algorithm" / "rules.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)["proofs"]["allow_prefixes"]

# why: the moment the frames are drawn at, a Tuesday evening, so "friday" is three days out
NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 18, 20, 14)

# Frame 3's seven rows, exactly as the contract table prints them.
FRAME_3_ROWS: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = [
    ("R1", "works on mobile", "screenshot 375px · no h-scroll", "○"),
    ("R2", "email-only login", "test_auth.py::magic_link", "○"),
    ("R3", "$47 everywhere · no $29", r'grep "\$29" src → 0', "○"),
    ("R4", "checkout untouched", "git diff --stat checkout/ → 0", "○"),
    ("R5", "skool opens new tab", "Nav.tsx target=_blank", "○"),
    ("R6", "feels premium", "undefined · pick a measurable", "?"),
    ("R7", "due friday 21 aug", "T-2d 19h", "◐"),
]

# The kind each of those rows lands on: two commands the gate may run, three checks a person makes,
# one requirement with no measurable, one deadline.
FRAME_3_KINDS = ["manual", "manual", "auto", "auto", "manual", "undefined", "date"]

TITLE = "members area"
SESSION = "session-one"


def seed_counter(state_dir: Path, value: str) -> None:
    """A state folder that already carries contracts, for the seeded half of each twin."""
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "counter").write_text(value, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")


def build(rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] | None = None, **kwargs: object) -> dict:
    """Frame 3's contract on disk, unless the test wants its own rows."""
    return ledger.from_table_rows(
        FRAME_3_ROWS if rows is None else rows,
        str(kwargs.pop("title", TITLE)),
        str(kwargs.pop("session_id", SESSION)),
        ALLOW_PREFIXES,
        contract_id=kwargs.pop("contract_id", None),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        now=NOW,
    )


def row_of(data: dict, row_id: str) -> dict:
    """One row of a loaded ledger, by number."""
    return next(row for row in data["rows"] if row["id"] == row_id)


def events(row: dict) -> list[str]:
    """The row's history as a list of what happened, in order."""
    return [entry["event"] for entry in row["history"]]


# ── numbering: the cold-start twins ──


def test_a_cold_start_writes_contract_one_and_moves_the_counter(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert ledger.next_id() == "0001"

    written = build()

    assert written["contract_id"] == "0001"
    assert ledger.counter_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "1"
    assert ledger.next_id() == "0002"
    assert ledger.load()["contract_id"] == "0001"


def test_a_seeded_counter_makes_the_next_contract_forty_two(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    seed_counter(state_dir, "0041")

    assert ledger.next_id() == "0042"

    written = build()

    assert written["contract_id"] == "0042"
    assert ledger.counter_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "42"
    assert ledger.next_id() == "0043"


def test_reading_the_next_number_never_burns_it(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    seed_counter(state_dir, "41")

    assert [ledger.next_id() for _ in range(3)] == ["0042", "0042", "0042"]


def test_a_printed_contract_id_wins_over_the_counter(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    seed_counter(state_dir, "0041")

    written = build(contract_id="0100")

    assert written["contract_id"] == "0100"
    assert ledger.counter_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "100"
    assert ledger.next_id() == "0101"


def test_a_counter_holding_words_reads_as_no_contracts(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    seed_counter(state_dir, "forty one")

    assert ledger.next_id() == "0001"


# ── kinds ──


def test_classify_kind_reads_frame_three_row_by_row(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    kinds = [
        ledger.classify_kind(text, proof, glyph, ALLOW_PREFIXES)
        for _, text, proof, glyph in FRAME_3_ROWS
    ]

    assert kinds == FRAME_3_KINDS


def test_the_allowlist_matches_whole_words_only(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    auto = 'pytest tests/test_auth.py -q'
    assert ledger.classify_kind("email-only login", auto, "", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "auto"
    assert ledger.classify_kind("email-only login", "npm test", "", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "auto"
    assert ledger.classify_kind("email-only login", "npm install", "", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "manual"
    assert ledger.classify_kind("email-only login", "pythonic check", "", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "manual"
    assert ledger.classify_kind("email-only login", "rm -rf /", "", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "manual"


def test_a_row_with_no_measurable_is_undefined_whatever_its_proof_says(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert ledger.classify_kind("feels premium", "pytest -q", "?", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "undefined"
    assert (
        ledger.classify_kind("feels premium", "undefined · pick a measurable", "", ALLOW_PREFIXES)
        == "undefined"
    )


def test_a_deadline_is_a_date_row_however_it_is_written(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    for text, proof in (
        ("due friday 21 aug", "T-2d 19h"),
        ("ships tomorrow", "a look at the page"),
        ("live by 2026-09-01", "a look at the page"),
        ("monday launch", "a look at the page"),
    ):
        assert ledger.classify_kind(text, proof, "", ALLOW_PREFIXES) == "date"


def test_the_kinds_are_written_into_the_rows(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    written = build()

    assert [row["kind"] for row in written["rows"]] == FRAME_3_KINDS
    assert [row["status"] for row in written["rows"]] == ["○", "○", "○", "○", "○", "?", "◐"]
    assert all(row["struck"] is False for row in written["rows"])
    assert all(events(row) == ["created"] for row in written["rows"])
    assert written["due"] == "2026-08-21T17:00:00"
    assert written["approved_at"] is None
    assert written["session_id"] == SESSION
    assert written["touched_outside"] == []


# ── the status by operation grid ──


def test_a_strike_on_a_proven_row_keeps_the_row_and_its_history(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.stamp("R1", "●", "screenshot taken", now=NOW)

    said = ledger.apply_edit("strike R1", now=NOW)

    assert said == "R1 struck"
    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R1")
    assert row["struck"] is True
    assert row["status"] == "●"  # why: striking removes the row from the contract, it does not unprove it
    assert events(row) == ["created", "proof", "struck"]
    assert row["text"] == "works on mobile"


def test_a_reword_on_a_failed_row_resets_it_to_open(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.stamp("R3", "✗", "3 hits", now=NOW)

    said = ledger.apply_edit("R3: $47 everywhere, nothing left at $29", now=NOW)

    assert said == "R3 reworded"
    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R3")
    assert row["status"] == "○"
    assert row["text"] == "$47 everywhere, nothing left at $29"
    assert row["proof"] == r'grep "\$29" src → 0'  # why: the reword changes the words, not the measure
    assert events(row) == ["created", "proof", "reworded"]


def test_a_reword_keeps_an_undefined_row_undefined(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    assert ledger.apply_edit("reword R6 premium type scale", now=NOW) == "R6 reworded"

    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R6")
    assert row["status"] == "?"
    assert row["text"] == "premium type scale"


def test_a_reword_brings_a_struck_row_back(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.apply_edit("strike R5", now=NOW)

    assert ledger.apply_edit("R5: skool opens in a new tab", now=NOW) == "R5 reworded"

    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R5")
    assert row["struck"] is False
    assert row["status"] == "○"
    assert events(row) == ["created", "struck", "reworded"]


def test_go_is_said_once(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.apply_edit("strike R6", now=NOW)

    assert ledger.approve(SESSION, now=NOW) is True
    assert ledger.approve(SESSION, now=NOW) is False

    data = ledger.load()
    assert data["approved_at"] == "2026-08-18T20:14:00"
    assert events(row_of(data, "R1")) == ["created", "approved"]
    assert events(row_of(data, "R6")) == ["created", "struck"]  # why: a struck row is not part of the deal


def test_approve_on_an_empty_state_folder_says_no(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert ledger.approve(SESSION, now=NOW) is False


def test_a_stamp_writes_the_status_the_output_and_the_moment(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    ledger.stamp("R4", "✗", "checkout/Button.tsx | 2 +-", now=NOW)

    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R4")
    assert row["status"] == "✗"
    assert row["last_output"] == "checkout/Button.tsx | 2 +-"
    assert row["last_run_at"] == "2026-08-18T20:14:00"
    assert row["history"][-1] == {
        "at": "2026-08-18T20:14:00",
        "event": "proof",
        "text": "checkout untouched",
        "status": "✗",
    }


def test_a_stamp_on_a_row_that_is_not_there_is_logged_not_raised(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    ledger.stamp("R9", "●", "", now=NOW)

    assert "R9" in (state_dir / "hooks.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert len(ledger.load()["rows"]) == len(FRAME_3_ROWS)


def test_rows_open_holds_everything_not_yet_proven(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.stamp("R1", "●", "", now=NOW)
    ledger.apply_edit("strike R2", now=NOW)

def test_the_edit_spellings_the_member_uses(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert ledger.is_edit("strike R3") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("strike 3") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("reword R3 works on tablets too") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("R3: works on tablets too") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("R3: works on tablets too · proof: pytest tests -q") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("reword R3 works on tablets too · proof: pytest tests -q") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("R3 proof: pytest tests -q") is True
    assert ledger.is_edit("R3 looks fine to me") is False
    assert ledger.is_edit("where are we on the members area") is False
    assert ledger.is_edit("go") is False

    assert ledger.apply_edit("strike R1", now=NOW) is None  # why: no contract to edit yet


# ── edits that change the proof (the walk's frame 3a) ──


def test_a_reword_carrying_a_proof_changes_both_and_says_so(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    """The walk's own line: the member re-states R2 and gives it a proof that runs here."""
    build()

    said = ledger.apply_edit(
        "reword R2 email-only login · proof: python -m pytest demo/site -q", now=NOW
    )

    assert said == "R2 reworded and reproofed"
    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R2")
    assert row["text"] == "email-only login"
    assert row["proof"] == "python -m pytest demo/site -q"
    assert row["kind"] == "auto"  # why: the new proof is allowlisted, so the gate may run it
    assert row["status"] == "○"
    assert events(row) == ["created", "reworded", "reproofed"]


def test_the_colon_spelling_carries_a_proof_too(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    said = ledger.apply_edit("R1: works on mobile at 375px · proof: grep 480px src → 0", now=NOW)

    assert said == "R1 reworded and reproofed"
    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R1")
    assert row["text"] == "works on mobile at 375px"
    assert row["proof"] == "grep 480px src → 0"
    assert row["kind"] == "auto"
    assert events(row) == ["created", "reworded", "reproofed"]


def test_a_proof_only_edit_leaves_the_wording_alone(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    said = ledger.apply_edit("R2 proof: python -m pytest demo/site -q", now=NOW)

    assert said == "R2 reproofed"
    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R2")
    assert row["text"] == "email-only login"  # why: the member changed the measure, not the promise
    assert row["proof"] == "python -m pytest demo/site -q"
    assert row["kind"] == "auto"
    assert events(row) == ["created", "reproofed"]


def test_a_new_proof_flips_the_kind_both_ways(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    assert ledger.apply_edit("R2 proof: pytest tests/test_auth.py -q", now=NOW) == "R2 reproofed"
    assert row_of(ledger.load(), "R2")["kind"] == "auto"  # why: manual before, the gate runs it now

    assert ledger.apply_edit("R3 proof: a look at the pricing page", now=NOW) == "R3 reproofed"
    assert row_of(ledger.load(), "R3")["kind"] == "manual"  # why: auto before, a person checks it now


def test_giving_an_undefined_row_a_measurable_makes_it_countable(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    said = ledger.apply_edit(
        "reword R6 premium type scale · proof: python -m pytest demo/site -q", now=NOW
    )

    assert said == "R6 reworded and reproofed"
    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R6")
    assert row["kind"] == "auto"
    assert row["status"] == "○"  # why: it was "?" with no measurable; now it is owed like any row


def test_a_reproofed_row_is_owed_again(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.stamp("R3", "●", "0 hits", now=NOW)

    ledger.apply_edit("R3 proof: grep -r $29 demo/site → 0", now=NOW)

    row = row_of(ledger.load(), "R3")
    assert row["status"] == "○"  # why: a different measure has not been run, so it is not proven
    assert events(row) == ["created", "proof", "reproofed"]

def test_no_real_prompt_is_read_as_an_edit(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    """The router asks ``is_edit`` of every prompt, so the grammar may never claim an ordinary one."""
    lines = (WS_ROOT / "tests" / "golden.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    prompts = [json.loads(line)["prompt"] for line in lines if line.strip()]

    assert len(prompts) > 100  # why: a shrunken golden set would make this sweep pass on nothing
    assert [prompt for prompt in prompts if ledger.is_edit(prompt)] == []

def test_the_lite_lines_read_the_counts_out(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    seed_counter(state_dir, "0041")
    build(
        [
            ("R1", "works on mobile", "screenshot 375px · no h-scroll", ""),
            ("R2", "email-only login", "pytest tests/test_auth.py -q", ""),
            ("R3", "$47 everywhere · no $29", r'grep "\$29" src → 0', ""),
            ("R4", "checkout untouched", "git diff --stat checkout/ → 0", ""),
            ("R5", "skool opens new tab", "grep target=_blank src/Nav.tsx", ""),
            ("R6", "feels premium", "a walk through the pricing page", ""),
            ("R7", "due friday 21 aug", "T-2d 19h", ""),
        ]
    )
    for row_id in ("R1", "R2", "R3", "R4", "R5"):
        ledger.stamp(row_id, "●", "", now=NOW)

    assert ledger.lite_lines(NOW) == [
        "contract #0042 · members area",
        "5 ● proven · 1 ○ open · 1 ◐ on track",
    ]

    # the manual mark is its own word, so a manual row reads "1 manual", never "1 manual manual"
    ledger.stamp("R1", "manual", "", now=NOW)
    assert ledger.lite_lines(NOW)[1] == "4 ● proven · 1 ○ open · 1 ◐ on track · 1 manual"


def test_the_summary_counts_only_what_is_live(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    seed_counter(state_dir, "0041")
    build()
    for row_id in ("R1", "R2", "R4", "R5"):
        ledger.stamp(row_id, "●", "", now=NOW)
    ledger.stamp("R3", "✗", "3 hits", now=NOW)  # why: the gate caught it once
    ledger.stamp("R3", "●", "0 hits", now=NOW)  # why: and it was fixed before the second run
    ledger.apply_edit("strike R6", now=NOW)
    ledger.approve(SESSION, now=NOW)

    data = ledger.summary()

    assert data == {
        "contract_id": "0042",
        "title": "members area",
        "total": 6,
        "built": 6,
        "proven": 5,
        "open": 0,
        "approved": True,
        "due": "2026-08-21T17:00:00",
        "catches": 1,
    }


# ── dates ──


def test_due_from_text_reads_the_words_a_member_uses(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert ledger.due_from_text("due friday", NOW) == datetime(2026, 8, 21, 17, 0)
    assert ledger.due_from_text("due friday 21 aug", NOW) == datetime(2026, 8, 21, 17, 0)
    assert ledger.due_from_text("ships tomorrow", NOW) == datetime(2026, 8, 19, 17, 0)
    assert ledger.due_from_text("live by 2026-09-01", NOW) == datetime(2026, 9, 1, 17, 0)
    assert ledger.due_from_text("done eod", NOW) == datetime(2026, 8, 18, 17, 0)
    assert ledger.due_from_text("due tuesday", NOW) == datetime(2026, 8, 18, 17, 0)
    assert ledger.due_from_text("works on mobile", NOW) is None
    assert ledger.due_from_text("", NOW) is None


def test_tminus_counts_down_and_then_stops(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert ledger.tminus(datetime(2026, 8, 21, 17, 0), NOW) == "T-2d 20h"
    assert ledger.tminus(datetime(2026, 8, 19, 5, 0), NOW) == "T-8h"
    assert ledger.tminus(NOW - timedelta(hours=1), NOW) == "T-0h"
    assert ledger.tminus(None, NOW) == ""
    assert ledger.tminus("2026-08-21T17:00:00", NOW) == "T-2d 20h"


# ── a file that cannot be read ──


def test_a_corrupt_ledger_is_moved_aside_and_read_as_none(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "ledger.json").write_text('{"contract_id": "0042", "rows": [', encoding="utf-8")

    assert ledger.load() is None

    moved = list(state_dir.glob("ledger.bad.*.json"))
    assert len(moved) == 1
    assert moved[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8").startswith('{"contract_id": "0042"')
    assert not (state_dir / "ledger.json").exists()
    assert "ledger.json did not read" in (state_dir / "hooks.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert ledger.view() == ""
    assert ledger.summary() is None


def test_a_ledger_holding_a_list_is_moved_aside_too(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "ledger.json").write_text("[]", encoding="utf-8")

    assert ledger.load() is None
    assert len(list(state_dir.glob("ledger.bad.*.json"))) == 1


def test_a_new_contract_writes_cleanly_over_a_quarantined_one(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (state_dir / "ledger.json").write_text("not json at all", encoding="utf-8")
    assert ledger.load() is None

    build()

    assert ledger.load()["contract_id"] == "0001"


# ── the table printed a second time ──


def test_an_unchanged_row_keeps_its_status_a_changed_one_resets_a_gone_one_is_struck(
    state_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.stamp("R1", "●", "screenshot taken", now=NOW)
    ledger.stamp("R3", "✗", "3 hits", now=NOW)
    reprinted = [
        FRAME_3_ROWS[0],  # R1 unchanged
        FRAME_3_ROWS[1],
        ("R3", "$47 everywhere, checkout included", r'grep "\$29" src → 0', ""),
        FRAME_3_ROWS[3],
        FRAME_3_ROWS[4],
        FRAME_3_ROWS[5],
        ("R8", "pricing page loads fast", "curl http://localhost:8080 -o /dev/null", ""),
    ]

    updated = ledger.update_from_table_rows(
        ledger.load(), reprinted, now=NOW, allow_prefixes=ALLOW_PREFIXES
    )

    assert row_of(updated, "R1")["status"] == "●"
    assert events(row_of(updated, "R1")) == ["created", "proof"]
    assert row_of(updated, "R3")["status"] == "○"
    assert row_of(updated, "R3")["text"] == "$47 everywhere, checkout included"
    assert events(row_of(updated, "R3")) == ["created", "proof", "reworded"]
    assert row_of(updated, "R7")["struck"] is True
    assert events(row_of(updated, "R7")) == ["created", "struck"]
    assert row_of(updated, "R8")["kind"] == "auto"
    assert row_of(updated, "R8")["status"] == "○"
    assert ledger.load()["rows"] == updated["rows"]  # why: the update saves, the caller need not


def test_a_reprinted_table_does_not_bring_back_a_struck_row(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.apply_edit("strike R2", now=NOW)

    updated = ledger.update_from_table_rows(
        ledger.load(), FRAME_3_ROWS, now=NOW, allow_prefixes=ALLOW_PREFIXES
    )

    row = row_of(updated, "R2")
    assert row["struck"] is True
    assert events(row) == ["created", "struck"]


def test_a_struck_row_is_left_alone_however_the_table_comes_back(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()
    ledger.apply_edit("strike R2", now=NOW)
    reworded = ("R2", "login by email link only", "test_auth.py::magic_link", "")

    once = ledger.update_from_table_rows(
        ledger.load(),
        [row for row in FRAME_3_ROWS if row[0] != "R2"],  # why: the model drops the row it was told to
        now=NOW,
        allow_prefixes=ALLOW_PREFIXES,
    )
    twice = ledger.update_from_table_rows(
        ledger.load(),
        [reworded] + [row for row in FRAME_3_ROWS if row[0] != "R2"],
        now=NOW,
        allow_prefixes=ALLOW_PREFIXES,
    )

    assert events(row_of(once, "R2")) == ["created", "struck"]  # why: struck once, not struck again
    row = row_of(twice, "R2")
    assert row["struck"] is True
    assert row["text"] == "email-only login"  # why: only the member's own reword brings a row back
    assert events(row) == ["created", "struck"]


def test_the_update_reads_the_shipped_allowlist_when_the_caller_passes_none(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    build()

    updated = ledger.update_from_table_rows(
        ledger.load(),
        FRAME_3_ROWS + [("R8", "auth passes", "pytest tests/test_auth.py -q", "")],
        now=NOW,
    )

    assert row_of(updated, "R8")["kind"] == "auto"



================================================
FILE: tests/test_proofs.py
