def test_build_in_flight_edge_everything_proven() -> None:
    """An approved contract with nothing left to prove is not in flight."""
    _write_ledger([{"id": "R1", "status": "●", "text": "works on mobile"}], approved=True)
    assert signals.read_state().build_in_flight is False


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- read_state


def test_read_state_without_a_ledger() -> None:
    assert signals.read_state() == State()


def test_read_state_approved_with_work_left() -> None:
    _write_ledger(
        [
            {"id": "R1", "status": "●", "text": "works on mobile"},
            {"id": "R2", "status": "◐", "text": "email-only login"},
            {"id": "R3", "status": "○", "text": "$47 everywhere"},
        ],
        approved=True,
    )
    assert signals.read_state() == State(
        ledger_on_disk=True, ledger_approved=True, build_in_flight=True
    )


def test_read_state_ledger_not_yet_approved() -> None:
    _write_ledger([{"id": "R1", "status": "○", "text": "works on mobile"}], approved=False)
    assert signals.read_state() == State(ledger_on_disk=True)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- tokens and clauses


def test_tokens_lowercases_and_keeps_punctuation() -> None:
    assert signals.tokens("Ok, the members area?") == ["ok,", "the", "members", "area?"]


def test_tokens_normalises_the_curly_apostrophe() -> None:
    assert signals.tokens("Don’t touch checkout") == ["don't", "touch", "checkout"]


def test_clauses_splits_the_rant_into_seven() -> None:
    assert signals.clauses(RANT) == [
        "ok the members area needs to work on mobile",
        "email-only login",
        "$47 everywhere no $29 left",
        "don't touch checkout",
        "skool opens a new tab",
        "feels premium",
        "due friday",
    ]


def test_clauses_splits_on_and_and_then() -> None:
    assert signals.clauses("keep it short and make it blue") == ["keep it short", "make it blue"]
    assert signals.clauses("build it then run the tests") == ["build it", "run the tests"]


def test_clauses_of_nothing_is_empty() -> None:
    assert signals.clauses("   ") == []


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- all_signals


def _named_by_the_rules() -> set[str]:
    """Every signal name the shipped rules refer to, read straight from the file."""
    names: set[str] = set()
    for spec in RULES["skins"].values():
        names.update(spec["signals"])
    return names


def test_all_signals_returns_exactly_the_names_the_rules_use() -> None:
    values = signals.all_signals(FRAME_1, NO_STATE, RULES)
    assert set(values) == _named_by_the_rules()


def test_all_signals_has_a_function_for_every_name_the_rules_use() -> None:
    assert _named_by_the_rules() <= set(signals.SIGNALS)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "prompt",
    [FRAME_1, FRAME_2, RANT, "go", "receipt", "oh fuck you claude", "thanks", ""],
)
def test_all_signals_stay_in_range_except_the_clause_count(prompt: str) -> None:
    values = signals.all_signals(prompt, State(ledger_on_disk=True), RULES)
    for name, value in values.items():
        if name == "obligation_clauses":
            assert value >= 0.0
        else:
            assert 0.0 <= value <= 1.0, f"{name} returned {value}"


def test_all_signals_matches_the_functions_called_one_by_one() -> None:
    state = State(ledger_on_disk=True, ledger_approved=True, build_in_flight=True)
    values = signals.all_signals(RANT, state, RULES)
    for name, value in values.items():
        assert value == signals.SIGNALS[name](RANT, state, LEXICONS)


def test_all_signals_counts_the_rant_clauses() -> None:
    assert signals.all_signals(RANT, NO_STATE, RULES)["obligation_clauses"] == 7.0


def test_all_signals_scores_an_unknown_name_at_zero() -> None:
    """A rules file naming a signal this build does not have keeps running, at nothing."""
    made_up = {"skins": {"x": {"signals": {"not_a_signal": 1.0}}}, "lexicons": LEXICONS}
    assert signals.all_signals("anything", NO_STATE, made_up) == {"not_a_signal": 0.0}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the pinned readings


def test_pinned_readings() -> None:
    """The exact numbers the plan pins, in one place, so a tuning change has to face them."""
    assert signals.progress_shape("how's it going", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert signals.decision_shape("how's it going", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 0.0
    assert signals.obligation_clauses(RANT, NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 7.0
    assert signals.obligation_clauses("keep it short and make it blue", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) <= 2.0
    assert signals.insult_at_model("this code is stupid", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 0.0
    assert signals.insult_at_model("you're useless", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert signals.go_word("go", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert signals.go_word("go on then", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 0.0
    assert signals.ack_or_rating("thanks", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert signals.ack_or_rating("8/10", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert (
        signals.ack_or_rating("thanks for the detailed rundown of everything", NO_STATE, LEXICONS)
        == 0.0
    )
    assert signals.date_mention("due friday", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert signals.question_shape("should we", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0
    assert signals.decision_shape("should I post on tiktok or instagram", NO_STATE, LEXICONS) == 1.0



================================================
FILE: tests/test_skins.py
================================================
"""The eight skins: their files, their headers, their table, and the text the router injects.

Two invariants are swept over all eight rather than sampled: the injection stays inside the
byte budget with every line inside 88 columns, and it never mentions the router past its own
first two lines. The contract table is driven the way the gate will read it: render seven
rows, parse them back with the gate's own regex, and byte-compare the signed frame.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

# harness fallback: tests/conftest.py puts <WS>/.claude/hooks on sys.path; this insert is a
# no-op once it has, and keeps `from lib import skins` resolving when a file is run alone.
_HOOKS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / ".claude" / "hooks"
if str(_HOOKS_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HOOKS_DIR))

from lib import skins  # noqa: E402
from lib.hookio import ROUTER_MAX_BYTES  # noqa: E402

try:  # a stand-in with the same fields, should lib.score be absent
    from lib.score import Decision
except ImportError:

    @dataclass
    class Decision:  # type: ignore[no-redef]
        """The routing decision the injection is built from."""

        skin: str
        tier: str
        score: float
        source: str
        signals_top3: list[tuple[str, float]] = field(default_factory=list)
        tie_with: str | None = None


SKIN_NAMES = ("banter", "bars", "bluf", "board", "contract", "minimal", "receipt", "sitrep")

# The two signed headers, copied verbatim from the frames script.
BLUF = """\
██████╗ ██╗     ██╗   ██╗███████╗
██╔══██╗██║     ██║   ██║██╔════╝
██████╔╝██║     ██║   ██║█████╗
██╔══██╗██║     ██║   ██║██╔══╝
██████╔╝███████╗╚██████╔╝██║
╚═════╝ ╚══════╝ ╚═════╝ ╚═╝"""

SITREP = """\
███████╗██╗████████╗██████╗ ███████╗██████╗
██╔════╝██║╚══██╔══╝██╔══██╗██╔════╝██╔══██╗
███████╗██║   ██║   ██████╔╝█████╗  ██████╔╝
╚════██║██║   ██║   ██╔══██╗██╔══╝  ██╔═══╝
███████║██║   ██║   ██║  ██║███████╗██║
╚══════╝╚═╝   ╚═╝   ╚═╝  ╚═╝╚══════╝╚═╝"""

# Frame C's seven rows and the table the frames script draws from them.
FRAME_C_ROWS = [
    ["R1", "works on mobile", "screenshot 375px · no h-scroll", " ○"],
    ["R2", "email-only login", "test_auth.py::magic_link", " ○"],
    ["R3", "$47 everywhere · no $29", r'grep "\$29" src → 0', " ○"],
    ["R4", "checkout untouched", "git diff --stat checkout/ → 0", " ○"],
    ["R5", "skool opens new tab", "Nav.tsx target=_blank", " ○"],
    ["R6", "feels premium", "undefined · pick a measurable", " ?"],
    ["R7", "due friday 21 aug", "T-2d 19h", " ◐"],
]
FRAME_C_TABLE = "\n".join(
    [
        "╔════╤═══════════════════════════╤══════════════════════════════════╤═════╗",
        "║ #  │ requirement               │ proof                            │     ║",
        "╟────┼───────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────╢",
        "║ R1 │ works on mobile           │ screenshot 375px · no h-scroll   │  ○  ║",
        "║ R2 │ email-only login          │ test_auth.py::magic_link         │  ○  ║",
        r'║ R3 │ $47 everywhere · no $29   │ grep "\$29" src → 0              │  ○  ║',
        "║ R4 │ checkout untouched        │ git diff --stat checkout/ → 0    │  ○  ║",
        "║ R5 │ skool opens new tab       │ Nav.tsx target=_blank            │  ○  ║",
        "║ R6 │ feels premium             │ undefined · pick a measurable    │  ?  ║",
        "║ R7 │ due friday 21 aug         │ T-2d 19h                         │  ◐  ║",
        "╚════╧═══════════════════════════╧══════════════════════════════════╧═════╝",
    ]
)

# The gate's own row regex, copied from the build contract; the skin must feed it.
GATE_ROW = re.compile(r"^║\s*(R\d+)\s*│\s*(.+?)\s*│\s*(.+?)\s*│\s*([●○◐?✗⏱]|manual)?\s*║$")

LEDGER_VIEW = "\n".join(
    [
        " R1 ● works on mobile · proof: screenshot 375px · no h-scroll",
        " R2 ● email-only login · proof: pytest tests/test_auth.py::magic_link",
        ' R3 ● $47 everywhere · no $29 · proof: grep -r "$29" src → 0',
        " R4 ● checkout untouched · proof: git diff --stat -- checkout/ → 0",
        " R5 ● skool opens new tab · proof: grep target=_blank src/Nav.tsx",
        " R6 ○ feels premium · proof: undefined · pick a measurable",
        " R7 ◐ due friday 21 aug · proof: T-2d 19h",
    ]
)
LEDGER_SUMMARY = {
    "contract_id": "0042",
    "title": "members area",
    "total": 7,
    "built": 5,
    "proven": 5,
    "open": 2,
    "approved": True,
    "due": "2026-08-21T17:00:00",
    "catches": 1,
}
ACTIVITY = "▁▁▂▃▅▇▇▅▃▂▁▁▁▂▅█▇▅▂▁"
NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 18, 20, 14)

# why: the style is read here too, because the column law and the long-dash rule are the skins'
# own laws and the model only sees them if the style states them every turn
STYLE_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / ".claude" / "output-styles" / "router.md"

# why: the closed set of hook data a skin body may ask for; anything else in a body is a typo
CLOSED_PLACEHOLDERS = (
    "stamp",
    "clock",
    "tminus",
    "contract_id",
    "title",
    "title_upper",
    "built_bar",
    "proven_bar",
    "proven",
    "open",
    "catches",
    "activity",
    "tier",
    "ledger_rows",
)

LONG_DASH = "—"  # why: the one glyph the voice rules ban, named by codepoint so it reads plainly

SITREP_BOX_INTERIOR = 64  # why: the SITREP box draws 67 columns and spends "│ " and "│" on its borders
SITREP_LABEL_COLUMN = 8  # why: "open" and its four trailing spaces sit before the slot starts
SITREP_SLOT_MAX = SITREP_BOX_INTERIOR - SITREP_LABEL_COLUMN  # why: what is left for the model to write

# Fixed words each skin must carry through the render, byte for byte.
FIXED_LABELS = {
    "bluf": ("▸", "why", "changes it", "next", "confidence"),
    "sitrep": ("SITUATION", "risk", "CONTRACT", "MISSION", "EXECUTION", "DECISION", "NEXT", "activity"),
    "contract": (
        "requirement",
        "proof",
        "say go · strike or reword any line by number",
        "undefined · pick a measurable",
    ),
    "bars": ("PROVEN", "↳", "heartbeat"),
    "board": ("open", "building", "proven"),
    "receipt": ("GLITCH CAT CLUB", "PROVEN", "OPEN", "GATE CATCHES", "thanks for building with glitch"),
    "minimal": (),
    "banter": (),
}


def decision_for(name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> Decision:
    """A decision pointing at one skin, with that skin's own tier."""
    skin = skins.load_skin(name)
    fields: dict[str, Any] = {
        "skin": name,
        "tier": skin.tier,
        "score": 0.81,
        "source": "rules",
        "signals_top3": [("progress_shape", 0.6), ("ledger_on_disk", 0.15)],
    }
    fields.update(kwargs)
    return Decision(**fields)


def injection(name: str, view: str = LEDGER_VIEW, summary: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> str:
    """The text the router would inject for one skin, with a full seven-row ledger."""
    return skins.render_injection(
        decision_for(name),
        view,
        ACTIVITY,
        LEDGER_SUMMARY if summary is None else summary,
        "0042",
        NOW,
    )


def style_body() -> str:
    """The Router output style with its front matter stripped, as the model reads it."""
    lines = STYLE_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    close = lines.index("---", 1)
    return "\n".join(lines[close + 1 :]).strip()


def flat(text: str) -> str:
    """One line with every run of whitespace collapsed, so a wrapped note still matches."""
    return " ".join(text.split())


def box_widths(text: str) -> list[list[int]]:
    """The drawn width of every line of every single-column box in ``text``, block by block."""
    groups: list[list[int]] = []
    current: list[int] | None = None
    for line in text.split("\n"):
        drawn = line.strip()
        if drawn.startswith("┌") and drawn.endswith("┐") and "┬" not in drawn:
            current = [len(drawn)]
        elif current is None:
            continue
        elif drawn.startswith("│"):
            current.append(drawn.rfind("│") + 1)  # why: the box ends at its last │, any suffix is outside it
        elif drawn.startswith("├"):
            current.append(len(drawn))
        elif drawn.startswith("└"):
            current.append(len(drawn))
            groups.append(current)
            current = None
        else:
            current = None
    return groups


def test_the_repo_ships_the_eight_skins() -> None:
    assert skins.available_skins() == list(SKIN_NAMES)


def test_every_skin_carries_the_five_front_matter_keys() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        skin = skins.load_skin(name)
        assert skin.name == name
        assert skin.tier.startswith("E")
        assert skin.use.strip()
        assert skin.fence in skins.FENCES
        assert skin.body.strip()
        assert skin.header == skin.header.strip()


def test_minimal_is_the_only_unfenced_skin() -> None:
    unfenced = [name for name in SKIN_NAMES if skins.load_skin(name).fence == "none"]
    assert unfenced == ["minimal"]


def test_every_injection_fits_the_byte_budget() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        size = len(injection(name).encode("utf-8"))
        assert size < ROUTER_MAX_BYTES, f"{name} injects {size} bytes"


def test_no_injected_line_runs_past_88_columns() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        for line in injection(name).split("\n"):
            assert len(line) <= skins.MAX_COLUMNS, f"{name}: {len(line)} columns: {line}"


def test_every_placeholder_is_substituted() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        assert "{{" not in injection(name), f"{name} left a placeholder behind"


def test_the_never_mention_line_is_the_last_line_of_every_injection() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        assert injection(name).split("\n")[-1] == skins.NEVER_MENTION


def test_the_router_only_speaks_in_the_first_two_lines() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        lines = injection(name).split("\n")
        assert lines[0].startswith("[router] ")
        for offset, line in enumerate(lines[2:], start=2):
            assert "[router]" not in line, f"{name}: line {offset} names the router"
            assert "score=" not in line, f"{name}: line {offset} carries a score"


def test_an_empty_ledger_still_renders_every_skin() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        text = skins.render_injection(decision_for(name), "", "", None, "0001", NOW)
        assert "{{" not in text
        assert text.split("\n")[-1] == skins.NEVER_MENTION
        assert "Ledger #" not in text


def test_the_fixed_words_survive_byte_exact() -> None:
    for name, labels in FIXED_LABELS.items():
        text = injection(name)
        for label in labels:
            assert label in text, f"{name} lost the fixed word {label!r}"


def test_the_headers_match_the_signed_frames() -> None:
    assert skins.render_header("bluf") == BLUF
    assert skins.render_header("sitrep") == SITREP


def test_the_contract_header_is_the_cat_and_the_tier() -> None:
    header = skins.render_header("contract", decision_for("contract"))
    lines = header.split("\n")
    assert len(lines) == 3
    assert lines[0].endswith("▶ entering the algorithm ██████████ E4")
    assert lines[1].strip() == "( o.o )"
    assert lines[2].strip() == "> ^ <"


def test_the_skins_without_a_header_render_none() -> None:
    for name in ("bars", "minimal", "receipt", "board", "banter"):
        assert skins.render_header(name) == ""
        assert "Header (copy verbatim" not in injection(name)


def test_the_contract_table_matches_the_signed_frame() -> None:
    assert skins.dbl_table(FRAME_C_ROWS) == FRAME_C_TABLE


def test_the_gate_reads_back_every_row_the_skin_draws() -> None:
    drawn = skins.dbl_table(FRAME_C_ROWS).split("\n")
    parsed = [GATE_ROW.match(line) for line in drawn]
    rows = [match for match in parsed if match is not None]
    assert [match.group(1) for match in rows] == [row[0] for row in FRAME_C_ROWS]
    assert [match.group(2) for match in rows] == [row[1] for row in FRAME_C_ROWS]
    assert [match.group(3) for match in rows] == [row[2] for row in FRAME_C_ROWS]
    assert [match.group(4) for match in rows] == [row[3].strip() for row in FRAME_C_ROWS]


def test_the_contract_skin_draws_a_row_the_gate_can_read() -> None:
    rows = [line for line in injection("contract").split("\n") if GATE_ROW.match(line)]
    assert rows, "the contract shape shows no row in the gate's own format"


def test_a_table_cell_never_pushes_the_columns() -> None:
    drawn = skins.dbl_table([["R1", "a requirement far longer than its column", "a proof far longer than its column", " ○"]])
    widths = {len(line) for line in drawn.split("\n")}
    assert widths == {len(FRAME_C_TABLE.split("\n")[0])}


def test_a_box_draws_every_line_to_one_width() -> None:
    drawn = skins.box(["short", skins.SEPARATOR, "a line far longer than the box it sits inside"], 30, "TITLE")
    assert {len(line) for line in drawn.split("\n")} == {30}
    assert drawn.split("\n")[0].startswith("┌─ TITLE ")


def test_every_rendered_box_keeps_one_width() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        for block in box_widths(injection(name)):
            assert len(set(block)) == 1, f"{name} drew a box with widths {sorted(set(block))}"


def test_a_long_title_and_a_wide_ledger_never_break_a_box() -> None:
    summary = dict(LEDGER_SUMMARY)
    summary["title"] = "the members area, the pricing page and the whole checkout"
    summary["total"] = 12
    summary["built"] = 11
    view = LEDGER_VIEW + "\n R8 ○ a requirement whose text runs far past any column the box has · proof: none"
    for name in ("sitrep", "receipt"):
        text = skins.render_injection(decision_for(name), view, ACTIVITY, summary, "0042", NOW)
        for block in box_widths(text):
            assert len(set(block)) == 1
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            assert len(line) <= skins.MAX_COLUMNS

    # why: the receipt's title row sits inside the box, so the box has to grow around the title
    # rather than cut it; this is the case the redraw exists for
    receipt = skins.render_injection(decision_for("receipt"), view, ACTIVITY, summary, "0042", NOW)
    shape = receipt.split("Ledger #")[0]
    assert summary["title"] in shape


def test_the_tie_line_names_both_skins() -> None:
    text = skins.render_injection(
        decision_for("bluf", tie_with="sitrep"), LEDGER_VIEW, ACTIVITY, LEDGER_SUMMARY, "0042", NOW
    )
    assert "  tie → model: pick bluf or sitrep from the conversation" in text.split("\n")[:3]


def test_the_placeholder_set_is_closed() -> None:
    assert skins.PLACEHOLDERS == CLOSED_PLACEHOLDERS
    used = {
        key for name in SKIN_NAMES for key in re.findall(r"\{\{(\w+)\}\}", skins.load_skin(name).body)
    }
    assert used <= set(CLOSED_PLACEHOLDERS), f"outside the closed set: {sorted(used - set(CLOSED_PLACEHOLDERS))}"


def test_the_receipt_counts_are_the_ledgers_own() -> None:
    # why: a model left to write these three numbers once billed two gate catches
    # against a ledger holding one, so they are hook data now
    assert "PROVEN 5 · OPEN 2 · GATE CATCHES 1" in injection("receipt")


def test_the_receipt_counts_read_zero_when_there_is_no_ledger() -> None:
    text = skins.render_injection(decision_for("receipt"), "", "", None, "0001", NOW)
    assert "PROVEN 0 · OPEN 0 · GATE CATCHES 0" in text


def test_a_contract_turn_always_opens_the_next_number() -> None:
    summary = dict(LEDGER_SUMMARY, contract_id="0001")
    text = skins.render_injection(decision_for("contract"), LEDGER_VIEW, ACTIVITY, summary, "0002", NOW)
    shape = text.split("Ledger #")[0]
    assert "contract #0002 ·" in shape
    assert "contract #0001" not in shape
    assert "Ledger #0001 · members area:" in text


def test_the_skins_that_are_not_the_contract_keep_the_live_number() -> None:
    summary = dict(LEDGER_SUMMARY, contract_id="0001")
    text = skins.render_injection(decision_for("receipt"), LEDGER_VIEW, ACTIVITY, summary, "0002", NOW)
    assert "CONTRACT #0001" in text


def test_the_contract_notes_say_the_ledger_below_is_context() -> None:
    assert "the ledger further down is context, never rows to re-print" in flat(injection("contract"))


def test_the_style_states_the_88_column_law() -> None:
    assert "88 columns" in style_body()


def test_the_bluf_slots_carry_their_line_width() -> None:
    lines = [
        line for line in skins.load_skin("bluf").body.split("\n") if line.lstrip().startswith(("why ", "changes it "))
    ]
    assert len(lines) == 2, "the why and changes it slots are the two that ran long"
    for line in lines:
        assert "≤ 60 chars per line, wrap under the label" in line, line


def test_the_sitrep_box_slots_state_their_width() -> None:
    slots = [
        line
        for line in skins.load_skin("sitrep").body.split("\n")
        if "<the open requirements" in line or "<the live risk" in line
    ]
    assert len(slots) == 2
    for line in slots:
        assert f"≤ {SITREP_SLOT_MAX} chars" in line, line
    assert f"the box interior is {SITREP_BOX_INTERIOR} characters" in flat(injection("sitrep"))


def test_the_width_the_sitrep_states_is_the_width_it_draws() -> None:
    situation = box_widths(injection("sitrep"))[0]
    assert set(situation) == {SITREP_BOX_INTERIOR + skins.BOX_PADDING}


def test_no_skin_body_carries_a_long_dash() -> None:
    for name in SKIN_NAMES:
        assert LONG_DASH not in skins.load_skin(name).body, f"{name} carries a long dash"


def test_the_style_forbids_the_long_dash_by_name() -> None:
    body = style_body()
    assert f"never the long dash ({LONG_DASH}) anywhere in a reply" in body.lower()
    assert "use a comma, a colon" in body


def test_the_bars_notes_rule_the_manual_time_column() -> None:
    assert "manual rows print the word manual in the time column, never a dash" in flat(injection("bars"))


def test_an_unknown_placeholder_is_refused_by_name(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    stray = tmp_path / "skins"
    stray.mkdir()
    (stray / "bluf.md").write_text(
        "---\nname: bluf\nheader: BLUF\ntier: E2\nuse: a decision\nfence: text\n---\n{{nonsense}}\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
        newline="\n",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(skins, "skins_dir", lambda: stray)
    try:
        injection("bluf")
    except skins.SkinError as error:
        assert "nonsense" in str(error)
    else:  # pragma: no cover - the guard is the point of the test
        raise AssertionError("an unknown placeholder was shipped instead of refused")

FILE: tests/test_sparkline.py
================================================
"""The sparkline and the progress bar: length, scale, rounding, and the empty cases."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

# harness fallback: tests/conftest.py puts <WS>/.claude/hooks on sys.path; this insert is a
# no-op once it has, and keeps `from lib import sparkline` resolving when a file is run alone.
_HOOKS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / ".claude" / "hooks"
if str(_HOOKS_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HOOKS_DIR))

from lib import sparkline  # noqa: E402

LOW = sparkline.SPARK[0]
HIGH = sparkline.SPARK[-1]


def test_spark_draws_one_cell_per_count() -> None:
    for counts in ([], [0], [1, 2, 3], [4, 0, 9, 2, 7, 7, 1, 0, 0, 3]):
        assert len(sparkline.spark(counts)) == len(counts)


def test_spark_of_an_idle_window_is_all_low_blocks() -> None:
    assert sparkline.spark([0, 0, 0, 0]) == LOW * 4
    assert sparkline.spark([]) == ""


def test_spark_puts_the_busiest_bucket_at_the_top() -> None:
    drawn = sparkline.spark([0, 1, 5, 9, 2])
    assert drawn[3] == HIGH
    assert drawn[0] == LOW
    assert drawn.count(HIGH) == 1


def test_spark_scales_linearly_between_the_two_ends() -> None:
    # why: eight levels over a max of 7 puts each count on its own block, low to high
    assert sparkline.spark([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]) == sparkline.SPARK


def test_spark_treats_a_negative_count_as_none() -> None:
    assert sparkline.spark([-5, 0, 0]) == LOW * 3


def test_bar_fills_the_share_it_was_given() -> None:
    drawn = sparkline.bar(5, 7)
    assert len(drawn) == 14
    assert drawn.count(sparkline.FULL) == 10
    assert drawn == sparkline.FULL * 10 + sparkline.EMPTY * 4


def test_bar_rounds_half_up() -> None:
    # why: 1 of 2 over 5 cells is 2.5 cells, and the member should see the half count
    assert sparkline.bar(1, 2, cells=5).count(sparkline.FULL) == 3


def test_bar_with_no_rows_is_empty_not_an_error() -> None:
    assert sparkline.bar(0, 0) == sparkline.EMPTY * 14


def test_bar_ends_are_whole() -> None:
    assert sparkline.bar(0, 7) == sparkline.EMPTY * 14
    assert sparkline.bar(7, 7) == sparkline.FULL * 14
    assert sparkline.bar(9, 7) == sparkline.FULL * 14



================================================
FILE: tests/test_telemetry.py
================================================
"""What the router records, and the heartbeat it reads back.

Cold-start twins throughout: every case runs once against an empty ``.router/`` (the autouse
``state_dir`` fixture gives each test its own) and once against one this test seeded itself.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

# why: telemetry draws the heartbeat with lib/sparkline.py; without it on
# disk these tests have nothing to run against, so they say so rather than fail as a red.
pytest.importorskip(
    "lib.sparkline", reason="lib/sparkline.py is missing"
)

from lib import telemetry  # noqa: E402 — only importable once sparkline exists
from lib.score import Decision  # noqa: E402

EMPTY_CELL = "▁"  # why: the sparkline's lowest cell, what a fresh clone shows
FULL_CELL = "█"  # why: the sparkline's highest cell, what the busiest bucket shows
BUCKETS = 20  # why: the default width of the heartbeat, asserted here so a change is deliberate


def a_decision(skin: str = "sitrep") -> Decision:
    """One explicit decision, the shape the router hands telemetry."""
    return Decision(
        skin=skin,
        tier="E3",
        score=1.0,
        source="explicit",
        signals_top3=[("progress_shape", 0.6), ("ledger_on_disk", 0.15)],
    )


def test_runs_file_sits_in_the_state_dir(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    assert telemetry.runs_path() == state_dir / "runs.jsonl"


def test_append_then_recent_returns_it() -> None:
    telemetry.append(a_decision(), "s1")

    rows = telemetry.recent("s1")
    assert len(rows) == 1
    row = rows[0]
    assert row["session_id"] == "s1"
    assert row["event"] == "route"
    assert row["skin"] == "sitrep"
    assert row["tier"] == "E3"
    assert row["score"] == 1.0
    assert row["source"] == "explicit"
    assert row["signals"] == {"progress_shape": 0.6, "ledger_on_disk": 0.15}
    assert row["ts"]


def test_recent_is_per_session_and_capped() -> None:
    for _ in range(3):
        telemetry.append(a_decision(), "s1")
    telemetry.append(a_decision("bluf"), "s2")

    assert len(telemetry.recent("s1", n=2)) == 2
    assert [row["skin"] for row in telemetry.recent("s2")] == ["bluf"]
    assert telemetry.recent("nobody") == []


def test_activity_on_an_empty_state_dir_is_flat() -> None:
    assert telemetry.activity() == EMPTY_CELL * BUCKETS


def test_activity_puts_this_moment_in_the_last_bucket() -> None:
    for _ in range(3):
        telemetry.append(a_decision(), "s1")

    line = telemetry.activity()
    assert len(line) == BUCKETS
    assert line[-1] == FULL_CELL
    assert set(line[:-1]) == {EMPTY_CELL}


def test_the_file_is_utf8_jsonl_with_one_line_per_run(state_dir: Path) -> None:
    telemetry.append(a_decision(), "s1")
    telemetry.append(a_decision("banter"), "s1", extra={"note": "café ▁"})

    raw = (state_dir / "runs.jsonl").read_bytes()
    assert b"\r\n" not in raw  # why: LF everywhere, so a file written on Windows reads the same
    text = raw.decode("utf-8")
    lines = [line for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    assert len(lines) == 2
    records = [json.loads(line) for line in lines]
    assert [record["skin"] for record in records] == ["sitrep", "banter"]
    assert records[1]["note"] == "café ▁"


def test_append_never_raises_when_the_line_cannot_be_written(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    def refuse(*args: object, **kwargs: object) -> None:
        raise OSError("the disk said no")

    monkeypatch.setattr("builtins.open", refuse)
    telemetry.append(a_decision(), "s1")  # a telemetry failure costs a line, never the turn



================================================
FILE: tests/test_transcript.py
================================================
"""Reading the harness's own transcript file, including the shapes that would break a naive reader.

The fixture below is written as bytes on purpose: one line carries a byte sequence that is not
UTF-8, one line is not JSON at all, one line ends CRLF, and the two content shapes (a plain string
and a list of blocks) both appear. A reader that raised on any of those would cost the member the
turn the gate is checking.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path

from lib import transcript

# why: the harness writes the reply a moment after the Stop hook starts; half a second is longer
# than the flush ever takes and short enough to keep the suite quick.
LATE_ARRIVAL_SECONDS = 0.5

# why: the bounded wait is 1.75 seconds (8 reads a quarter of a second apart), so anything under
# three is proof it gave up rather than hanging the member's turn.
WAIT_CEILING_SECONDS = 3.0


def a_line(role: str, content: object, entry_type: str | None = None) -> bytes:
    """One transcript entry as the harness writes it: the message nested under ``message``."""
    entry = {
        "type": entry_type or role,
        "message": {"role": role, "content": content},
    }
    return json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8") + b"\n"


def a_transcript(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """The mixed fixture: both content shapes, an undecodable line, a non-JSON line, a CRLF line."""
    path = tmp_path / "session.jsonl"
    blocks = [
        {"type": "thinking", "thinking": "not this"},
        {"type": "text", "text": "the plan is one line"},
    ]
    path.write_bytes(
        a_line("user", "first question")
        + a_line("assistant", "a plain string reply")
        + b"{not json at all\n"
        + b'{"type": "assistant", "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": "\xff\xfe bad bytes"}}\n'
        + a_line("user", [{"type": "text", "text": "second question"}])
        + a_line("assistant", blocks).replace(b"\n", b"\r\n")
        + b"\n"
    )
    return path


def test_last_assistant_text_reads_the_last_block_shape(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(a_transcript(tmp_path)) == "the plan is one line"


def test_last_assistant_text_reads_a_plain_string(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "one.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("user", "hi") + a_line("assistant", "a plain string reply"))

    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(path) == "a plain string reply"


def test_last_assistant_text_keeps_the_line_breaks(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "table.jsonl"
    table = "║ R1 │ works │ pytest -q │ ○ ║\n║ R2 │ loads │ curl │ ○ ║"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("assistant", table))

    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(path) == table


def test_the_undecodable_and_unparseable_lines_never_surface(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = a_transcript(tmp_path)

    everything = transcript.recent_turns(path, n=10)

    assert "bad bytes" not in everything
    assert "not json" not in everything
    assert everything.count("\n") == 3  # why: four turns survive the two broken lines
    assert "bad bytes" not in transcript.last_assistant_text(path)


def test_last_assistant_text_is_empty_without_a_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(tmp_path / "missing.jsonl") == ""
    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(None) == ""
    assert transcript.last_assistant_text("") == ""


def test_last_assistant_text_is_empty_when_only_the_member_spoke(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "user-only.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("user", "hello"))

    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(path) == ""


# --- the reply belongs to the turn being checked --------------------------------------------


def test_a_reply_older_than_the_newest_prompt_is_not_this_turn(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """The member has asked again and the model has not answered yet, so there is no reply to read.

    The Stop gate would otherwise read the table above and treat it as this turn's, which is how a
    contract the member had already dealt with was captured a second time.
    """
    path = tmp_path / "mid-turn.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(
        a_line("user", "first question")
        + a_line("assistant", "the reply to the first question")
        + a_line("user", "second question")
    )

    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(path) == ""


def test_the_reply_reads_back_once_it_lands(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "landed.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(
        a_line("user", "first question")
        + a_line("assistant", "the reply to the first question")
        + a_line("user", "second question")
    )

    with open(path, "ab") as stream:
        stream.write(a_line("assistant", "the reply to the second question"))

    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(path) == "the reply to the second question"


def test_a_tool_result_between_the_prompt_and_the_reply_hides_nothing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """A tool result is written as a user entry, and the answer still comes after it."""
    path = tmp_path / "tools.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(
        a_line("user", "run the suite")
        + a_line("assistant", [{"type": "tool_use", "name": "Bash"}])
        + a_line("user", [{"type": "tool_result", "content": "468 passed"}])
        + a_line("assistant", "468 passed, nothing red")
    )

    assert transcript.last_assistant_text(path) == "468 passed, nothing red"


# --- wait_for_reply -------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_wait_for_reply_returns_the_text_when_it_arrives_late(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "late.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("user", "ok the members area needs to work on mobile"))

    def write_the_reply() -> None:
        time.sleep(LATE_ARRIVAL_SECONDS)
        with open(path, "ab") as stream:
            stream.write(a_line("assistant", "║ R1 │ works on mobile │ screenshot 375px │ ○ ║"))

    writer = threading.Thread(target=write_the_reply)
    started = time.monotonic()
    writer.start()
    try:
        text = transcript.wait_for_reply(path)
    finally:
        writer.join()
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - started

    assert text == "║ R1 │ works on mobile │ screenshot 375px │ ○ ║"
    assert elapsed < WAIT_CEILING_SECONDS


def test_wait_for_reply_gives_up_inside_its_budget(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "silent.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("user", "ok the members area needs to work on mobile"))

    started = time.monotonic()
    text = transcript.wait_for_reply(path)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - started

    assert text == ""
    assert elapsed >= LATE_ARRIVAL_SECONDS, "the wait is real, not a single look"
    assert elapsed < WAIT_CEILING_SECONDS, "a Stop hook has 60 seconds and must not spend them here"


def test_wait_for_reply_with_no_path_answers_at_once(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    started = time.monotonic()

    assert transcript.wait_for_reply(None) == ""
    assert transcript.wait_for_reply("") == ""

    assert time.monotonic() - started < LATE_ARRIVAL_SECONDS, "nothing named means nothing to wait for"


def test_wait_for_reply_reads_once_when_it_is_asked_for_one_attempt(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "one-look.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("user", "hello"))

    started = time.monotonic()
    text = transcript.wait_for_reply(path, attempts=1)

    assert text == ""
    assert time.monotonic() - started < LATE_ARRIVAL_SECONDS


def test_recent_turns_returns_the_last_two(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    lines = transcript.recent_turns(a_transcript(tmp_path), n=2).split("\n")

    assert lines == ["user: second question", "assistant: the plan is one line"]


def test_recent_turns_clips_each_turn(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "long.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("assistant", "x" * 900))

    line = transcript.recent_turns(path, n=1)

    assert line.startswith("assistant: ")
    assert len(line) == len("assistant: ") + 300


def test_recent_turns_puts_each_turn_on_one_line(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "multiline.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("assistant", "first line\nsecond line"))

    assert transcript.recent_turns(path) == "assistant: first line second line"


def test_recent_turns_skips_entries_that_are_not_turns(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "mixed.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(
        json.dumps({"type": "summary", "summary": "a title"}).encode("utf-8")
        + b"\n"
        + a_line("assistant", [{"type": "tool_use", "name": "Bash"}])
        + a_line("assistant", "the answer")
    )

    assert transcript.recent_turns(path) == "assistant: the answer"


def test_recent_turns_is_empty_without_a_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert transcript.recent_turns(tmp_path / "missing.jsonl") == ""
    assert transcript.recent_turns(None) == ""


def test_recent_turns_of_nothing_is_empty(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "one.jsonl"
    path.write_bytes(a_line("assistant", "the answer"))

    assert transcript.recent_turns(path, n=0) == ""

================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/gate.py
================================================
"""Stop hook: hold the reply to the contract the member agreed to.

Two jobs, and the reply decides which one runs. A reply that prints the contract table is the
contract being written down: the rows are read straight out of the table the member just saw, the
ledger is written or folded into the one already on disk, and nothing is said back. A reply that
claims the work is done, on a contract the member approved, is checked: every allowlisted proof
runs, each row is stamped with what happened, and a red row holds the turn open with one sentence
naming it.

Nothing executes until both facts are true, the member approved the contract and the command is on
the allowlist in ``rules.json``, so a Stop hook in a public repo can never run a string the member
has not read. ``stop_hook_active`` is honoured before anything else, so the gate says its piece
once and never argues with itself.

Whatever happens inside, the hook exits 0 and writes at most one thing to stdout: a gate that falls
over must cost the member nothing but an unchecked turn.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib
import re
import sys
import traceback
from typing import Any

# why: the hooks folder is the import root for `lib`, and a hook can be launched from any cwd
sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

# why: below the path line, which is what E402 is waived for; `rules` arrives as the module so a
# broken rules file can be swapped for the fallback by name rather than by re-importing
from lib import hookio, ledger, proofs, telemetry, transcript  # noqa: E402
from lib import rules as rules_lib  # noqa: E402
from lib.score import Decision  # noqa: E402

GATE_EVENT = "gate"  # why: the telemetry event name, and the word a gate line carries in place of a skin
GATE_TIER = ""  # why: the gate picks no tier, and an invented one would read as a routing decision
GATE_SCORE = 0.0  # why: same reason: nothing was scored here, so the line says zero rather than a guess
NO_SHELL_CHOSEN = ""  # why: no proof ran this turn, so no shell was chosen (proofs.NO_SHELL means none was found)

# why: the contract table's own shape, drawn by skins.dbl_table at fixed widths 4/27/34/5. The
# status cell is optional because a freshly printed table leaves it blank.
ROW_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^║\s*(R\d+)\s*│\s*(.+?)\s*│\s*(.+?)\s*│\s*([●○◐?✗⏱]|manual)?\s*║$", re.MULTILINE
)
SLOT_MARK = "<"  # why: a row still holding the skin's <slot> wording is the shape, not a requirement

# why: the number the reply printed is the number the member read, so it wins over the counter
ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"contract #(\d{4})")
TITLE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"contract #\d{4}\s*·\s*([^\n]*)")
TITLE_SEPARATOR = " · "  # why: the header line is "contract #0042 · members area · 7 heard"
UNTITLED = "untitled"  # why: a contract with no title still needs a word every injection can print

# why: the one shape that says the work is finished: the bars box the model draws when it has run
# everything ("✔ 5/5 PROVEN"), or the words a member would read as the same claim. The hooks print
# the word "proven" themselves, in the catch-up line ("5 ● proven · 1 ○ open") and on the receipt
# ("PROVEN 5 · OPEN 1"), so a looser pattern read the router's own counting as a claim and ran the
# proofs on a turn that claimed nothing.
CLAIM_BOX = r"✔\s*\d+\s*/\s*\d+\s*PROVEN"
CLAIM_WORDS = r"\ball (?:done|proven)\b"
CLAIM_PATTERN = re.compile(f"{CLAIM_BOX}|{CLAIM_WORDS}", re.IGNORECASE)

# why: a deadline is proven by the clock and an undefined row has nothing to run, so both keep
# their own mark when the gate reports back "manual" for them; a row the model wrote as a check a
# person makes is stamped `manual`, because that is what the gate learned about it.
KEEP_OWN_MARK = (ledger.KIND_DATE, ledger.KIND_UNDEFINED)

UNPROVEN_PART = "{row_id} unproven"  # why: an allowlisted proof that did not run leaves the row owed
FAILED_PART = "{row_id} failed"
FAILED_WITH_OUTPUT = "{row_id} failed: {output}"
TIMEOUT_PART = "{row_id} timed out"
MANUAL_PART = "manual: {ids}"  # why: the rows that are the member's to check, never the gate's to block on
TOUCHED_PART = "also changed, not named by any row: {paths}"  # why: a note for the member to eyeball, never an accusation of the work the contract asked for
PART_JOIN = " · "  # why: the separator the frames use inside a line, so the reason reads like the rest
LIST_JOIN = ", "
REASON_OUTPUT_CHARS = 120  # why: enough of a failure to recognise it, short enough to read in one line


def parse_contract_rows(text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:
    """Every contract row in ``text`` as ``(id, requirement, proof, status)``.

    The one regex the skin's fixed column widths exist for. A row whose requirement still starts
    with ``<`` is the skin's own shape rather than something the member agreed to, so it is skipped.
    """
    rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = []
    for match in ROW_PATTERN.finditer(text or ""):
        row_id, requirement, proof, status = match.groups()
        requirement = requirement.strip()
        if requirement.startswith(SLOT_MARK):
            continue
        rows.append((row_id, requirement, proof.strip(), (status or "").strip()))
    return rows


def _printed_id(text: str) -> str | None:
    """The contract number the reply printed, or ``None`` when it printed none."""
    found = ID_PATTERN.search(text or "")
    return found.group(1) if found else None


def _title(text: str) -> str:
    """The contract's title: what the header line says after the number, up to the next separator."""
    found = TITLE_PATTERN.search(text or "")
    if not found:
        return UNTITLED
    return found.group(1).split(TITLE_SEPARATOR)[0].strip() or UNTITLED


def _rules() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The shipped algorithm, or the fallback when the file is broken.

    The fallback ships an empty allowlist, so a rules file that will not load means every proof is
    left to the member rather than run under a rule nobody could read.
    """
    try:
        return rules_lib.load_rules()
    except rules_lib.RulesError as exc:
        hookio.log(f"gate: {exc}; carrying on with the fallback rules")
        return rules_lib.FALLBACK_RULES


def _allow_prefixes(rules: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    """The proof allowlist out of the rules, and nothing at all when it is not a list of strings."""
    settings = rules.get("proofs") if isinstance(rules, dict) else None
    prefixes = settings.get("allow_prefixes") if isinstance(settings, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(prefixes, list):
        return []
    return [item for item in prefixes if isinstance(item, str)]


def _cwd(data: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Where the proofs run: the session's own folder, or the repo root when it names none."""
    raw = str(data.get("cwd") or "").strip()
    if raw:
        path = pathlib.Path(raw)
        if path.is_dir():
            return str(path)
        hookio.log(f"gate: the payload names a cwd that is not a folder ({raw!r}); using the repo root")
    return str(hookio.repo_root())


def _text_of(value: Any) -> str:
    return str(value).strip() if value is not None else ""


def _live_rows(contract: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """The rows still on the contract, in the order the table printed them."""
    rows = contract.get("rows") if isinstance(contract, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(rows, list):
        return []
    return [row for row in rows if isinstance(row, dict) and not row.get("struck")]


def _row_kind(contract: dict[str, Any], row_id: str) -> str:
    for row in _live_rows(contract):
        if _text_of(row.get("id")) == row_id:
            return _text_of(row.get("kind"))
    return ""


def _contract_id(contract: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str:
    return _text_of(contract.get("contract_id")) if isinstance(contract, dict) else ""


def _capture(rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]], text: str, session_id: str, rules: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Write the table the reply printed into the ledger, and give back the contract number.

    A table carrying the number already on disk, or carrying no number at all, is the same contract
    said again, so it folds in and keeps what has been proven. A different number is a new contract.
    """
    allow = _allow_prefixes(rules)
    printed = _printed_id(text)
    existing = ledger.load()
    if existing is not None and (printed is None or _contract_id(existing) == printed):
        contract = ledger.update_from_table_rows(existing, rows, allow_prefixes=allow)
    else:
        contract = ledger.from_table_rows(rows, _title(text), session_id, allow, contract_id=printed)
    return _contract_id(contract)


def _stamp(contract: dict[str, Any], results: list[proofs.Result]) -> None:
    """Record what each proof did. A row the gate could not run keeps its own mark."""
    for result in results:
        if result.status == ledger.MANUAL_STATUS and _row_kind(contract, result.row_id) in KEEP_OWN_MARK:
            continue
        ledger.stamp(result.row_id, result.status, result.output)


def _snippet(output: str) -> str:
    """The head of a failure's output on one line, so the reason stays readable."""
    return " ".join(str(output or "").split())[:REASON_OUTPUT_CHARS]


def _reason(contract: dict[str, Any], touched: list[str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """The sentence the member reads, and whether it holds the turn open.

    Only a row the gate could have proven blocks: an allowlisted proof that failed or never
    ran; a timed-out proof is named but never blocks on its own. A manual row is named so the member knows it is theirs, and the files touched
    outside the contract ride on the end of a reason that was blocking anyway.
    """
    parts: list[str] = []
    manual: list[str] = []
    blocked = False
    for row in _live_rows(contract):
        row_id = _text_of(row.get("id"))
        status = _text_of(row.get("status"))
        provable = _text_of(row.get("kind")) == ledger.KIND_AUTO
        if status == ledger.MANUAL_STATUS:
            manual.append(row_id)
        elif status == ledger.FAILED_GLYPH:
            snippet = _snippet(_text_of(row.get("last_output")))
            parts.append(
                FAILED_WITH_OUTPUT.format(row_id=row_id, output=snippet)
                if snippet
                else FAILED_PART.format(row_id=row_id)
            )
            blocked = blocked or provable
        elif status == ledger.TIMEOUT_GLYPH:
            # why: a proof that overran is named, never blocked on (plan decision 2): a hung
            # command must not lock the member's turn, and the row stays visibly unproven
            parts.append(TIMEOUT_PART.format(row_id=row_id))
        elif status == ledger.OPEN_GLYPH and provable:
            parts.append(UNPROVEN_PART.format(row_id=row_id))
            blocked = True
    if manual:
        parts.append(MANUAL_PART.format(ids=LIST_JOIN.join(manual)))
    if touched:
        parts.append(TOUCHED_PART.format(paths=LIST_JOIN.join(touched)))
    return PART_JOIN.join(parts), blocked


def _record(contract_id: str, ran: int, blocked: bool, shell: str, session_id: str) -> None:
    """One telemetry line per gate run, so a session's Stop turns read back beside its routes."""
    telemetry.append(
        Decision(skin=GATE_EVENT, tier=GATE_TIER, score=GATE_SCORE, source=GATE_EVENT),
        session_id,
        event=GATE_EVENT,
        extra={"contract_id": contract_id, "ran": ran, "blocked": blocked, "shell": shell},
    )


def _prove(contract: dict[str, Any], data: dict[str, Any], rules: dict[str, Any], session_id: str) -> None:
    """Run the contract's proofs, stamp the ledger, and block once when something is still red."""
    cwd = _cwd(data)
    results = proofs.run_all(contract, cwd, rules)
    _stamp(contract, results)

    stamped = ledger.load() or contract
    touched = proofs.git_touched_outside(cwd, stamped)
    stamped["touched_outside"] = touched
    ledger.save(stamped)

    reason, blocked = _reason(stamped, touched)
    ran = sum(1 for result in results if result.status != ledger.MANUAL_STATUS)
    shell = results[0].shell if results else NO_SHELL_CHOSEN
    _record(_contract_id(stamped), ran, blocked, shell, session_id)
    if blocked:
        hookio.emit_block(reason)


def main() -> None:
    """Read the reply, capture the contract or check the claim, say at most one thing."""
    try:
        if hookio.is_child_process():
            sys.exit(0)

        data = hookio.read_hook_input()
        if data.get("stop_hook_active"):
            # why: the gate already spoke on this turn; blocking again would talk over itself
            sys.exit(0)

        session_id = str(data.get("session_id") or "")
        text = str(data.get("last_assistant_message") or "").strip()
        if not text:
            # why: the payload carries no reply on most Stop turns, and the transcript may still be
            # a moment behind, so the file is re-read for a couple of seconds before the gate calls
            # the turn empty; a table missed here is a contract the member agreed to and nobody wrote down
            text = transcript.wait_for_reply(data.get("transcript_path"))

        rules = _rules()
        rows = parse_contract_rows(text)
        if rows:
            _record(_capture(rows, text, session_id, rules), 0, False, NO_SHELL_CHOSEN, session_id)
            sys.exit(0)

        contract = ledger.load()
        if contract is None or not contract.get("approved_at") or not CLAIM_PATTERN.search(text):
            _record(_contract_id(contract), 0, False, NO_SHELL_CHOSEN, session_id)
            sys.exit(0)

        _prove(contract, data, rules, session_id)
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — the never-crash law: an unchecked turn beats a broken session
        hookio.log("gate: " + traceback.format_exc())
        sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/restore.py
================================================
"""SessionStart hook: hand the live contract back to a session that has just opened.

A ``/clear`` wipes the window, not the work. When a contract is on disk the new session opens
already knowing its number, its title, where every row stands and what is still owed, so the member
carries on in one sentence instead of re-stating the whole thing. With no contract on disk there is
nothing to restore and the hook says nothing at all.

Same law as the router: at most one thing on stdout, exit 0 whatever happens inside. A restore that
fell over would cost the member their session start, which is a far worse price than a window that
opens without the contract in it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib
import sys
import traceback
from typing import Any

# why: the hooks folder is the import root for `lib`, and a hook can be launched from any cwd
sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

# why: below the path line, which is what E402 is waived for
from lib import hookio, ledger  # noqa: E402

HOOK_EVENT = "SessionStart"  # why: the harness reads the event name back out of the envelope
RESTORED_LINE = "[router] restored contract #{contract_id}"  # why: the first line the session reads
LINE_JOIN = " · "  # why: the separator the frames use inside a data line, kept the same here
COUNT_PAIR = "{count} {glyph}"  # why: the head line counts the glyphs, the words come with the rows

# why: the session is told the contract is live and told to leave it alone until it is asked for,
# because a window that opens by re-printing the whole table is the thing /clear was run to escape
CARRY_ON = (
    "The contract above is live; carry on from it. Do not repeat it unless asked, "
    "and never mention the router."
)


def _live_rows(ledger_data: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """The rows that still stand: on the contract, and not struck by the member."""
    rows = ledger_data.get("rows")
    if not isinstance(rows, list):
        return []
    return [row for row in rows if isinstance(row, dict) and not row.get("struck")]


def _counts(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """``5 ● · 1 ○ · 1 ◐``: every glyph that has rows, in the ledger's own reading order."""
    tally: dict[str, int] = {}
    for row in rows:
        status = str(row.get("status") or "").strip()
        if status:
            tally[status] = tally.get(status, 0) + 1
    return LINE_JOIN.join(
        COUNT_PAIR.format(count=tally[glyph], glyph=glyph)
        for glyph, _word in ledger.COUNT_WORDS
        if tally.get(glyph)
    )


def _head_line(summary: dict[str, Any], rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """The one line that says which contract came back and how it stands."""
    parts = [RESTORED_LINE.format(contract_id=summary.get("contract_id") or ledger.next_id())]
    title = str(summary.get("title") or "").strip()
    if title:
        parts.append(title)
    counts = _counts(rows)
    if counts:
        parts.append(counts)
    return LINE_JOIN.join(parts)


def main() -> None:
    """Restore the contract into the new session, or say nothing at all."""
    try:
        if hookio.is_child_process():
            sys.exit(0)

        # why: the payload carries no field the restore reads, and the hook still drains stdin so
        # the harness is never left writing into a closed pipe
        hookio.read_hook_input()

        summary = ledger.summary()
        if summary is None:
            sys.exit(0)

        rows = _live_rows(ledger.load() or {})
        lines = [_head_line(summary, rows)]
        rendered = ledger.view()
        if rendered.strip():
            lines.append(rendered.strip("\n"))
        lines.append(CARRY_ON)

        # why: the same byte budget the router obeys; the head line survives any clip, so a session
        # that opens on an oversized contract still learns which contract it is on
        hookio.emit_context(HOOK_EVENT, hookio.clip_bytes("\n".join(lines), hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES))
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — the never-crash law: the session opens regardless
        hookio.log("restore: " + traceback.format_exc())
        sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/router.py
================================================
"""UserPromptSubmit hook: pick the skin for this turn and inject it.

The ways a turn can be answered, checked in this order: the house command the member typed
(``default sitrep``), a numbered edit to the contract (``strike R6``, ``R3: <new wording>``), the
word that starts the build (``go``), the catch-up question after a ``/clear``, the explicit token
they typed (``/e5``, ``receipt``), the classifier when the rules ask for one, and the scored signals
when they do not. Nothing matches the scored middle above the threshold, so the house floor or the
rules' default skin answers at 0.00, which is the honest reading rather than a low guess.

The three contract turns come first because each of them is the member saying something about the
contract by number or by name, which is a certainty; scoring a certainty could only get it wrong.

Whatever happens inside, the hook exits 0 and writes at most one thing to stdout: a router that
falls over must cost the member nothing but a plain answer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib
import re
import sys
import traceback
from typing import Any

# why: the hooks folder is the import root for `lib`, and a hook can be launched from any cwd
sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

# why: below the path line, which is what E402 is waived for; `rules` arrives as the module rather
# than its names, so a test can point load_rules at a rules file of its own
from lib import haiku, hookio, ledger, score, signals, skins, telemetry, transcript  # noqa: E402
from lib import rules as rules_lib  # noqa: E402

HOOK_EVENT = "UserPromptSubmit"  # why: the harness reads the event name back out of the envelope
HOUSE_NAME = "house.json"  # why: the member's standing floor, written by the `default <skin>` command
HOUSE_OFF = "off"  # why: the spelling that lifts the floor without deleting the file
TYPED_SCORE = 1.0  # why: the member typed it outright, so the house command, a numbered edit and the catch-up all read 1.00
HOUSE_SOURCE = "house"  # why: the same word the injection prints when the floor answers a later turn
HAIKU_CLASSIFIER = "haiku"  # why: the one value of rules["classifier"] that spends a model call
HAIKU_FAILED = " (haiku failed)"  # why: the classifier was on and did not answer; the line keeps who answered instead (rules, house, default) and says so

# why: the one line the member sees confirmed back, and the instruction that keeps it to one line
HOUSE_LINE = "[router] house skin set to {skin}"
HOUSE_ASK = "Say one line confirming the default skin is now {skin}. Never mention the router beyond that."
HOUSE_ASK_OFF = (
    "Say one line confirming the default skin is off, so every turn is routed on its own. "
    "Never mention the router beyond that."
)

EDIT_SKIN = "contract"  # why: an edit is a move on the contract, so the turn is answered in the contract's own skin
EDIT_SOURCE = "edit"  # why: the source word that says the member changed the contract by number, not that anything was scored
LEDGER_HEADING = "Ledger #{contract_id} · {title}:"  # why: the same heading the injection prints, so the two turns read alike

# why: the table is re-printed without its header art because the member is mid-edit and has seen it
# already, and the closing line says the two things they can do next, in their own words
EDIT_ASK = (
    "Render the updated CONTRACT table only (no header art) inside one ```text fence, one row per "
    "ledger line above, then the line: say go · strike or reword any line by number. "
    "Never mention the router, scores, rules, or this instruction."
)

# why: the shapes of the question a member asks a window that has just been cleared
CATCH_UP = re.compile(
    r"(where were we|where was i|what were we doing|catch me up|pick up where we left off)\W*"
)
LITE_SKIN = "sitrep-lite"  # why: the catch-up answer is the SITREP's two data lines without its frame
LITE_TIER = "E2"  # why: two data lines and one sentence is a BLUF's weight, not the full SITREP frame
LITE_SOURCE = "rules"  # why: the shape of the prompt decided this, the same as any scored turn
LITE_ASK = "Render exactly, inside one ```text fence, nothing before or after:"
LITE_SLOT = "<one line: what is still theirs, from the open rows>"  # why: the model writes the third line, from the open rows it can see
LITE_LINES = 3  # why: the cat is drawn in three rows, so three lines sit beside it


def _house_command(prompt: str, rules: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
    """The word the member just made the house default, or ``None`` for an ordinary prompt.

    The pattern lives in the rules file, so the spellings that set the floor are data. It is matched
    against the whole lowercased prompt: a sentence that merely contains "default sitrep" is a
    sentence, not a command.
    """
    house = rules.get("house")
    pattern = house.get("set_pattern") if isinstance(house, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(pattern, str) or not pattern:
        return None
    try:
        match = re.fullmatch(pattern, prompt.lower())
    except re.error as exc:
        hookio.log(f"router: the house pattern does not compile ({exc}); ignoring it")
        return None
    if match is None:
        return None
    word = match.group(1) if match.groups() and match.group(1) else prompt.lower().split()[-1]
    return word or None


def _set_house(word: str, rules: dict[str, Any], session_id: str) -> None:
    """Write the floor, confirm it in one line, record it. The whole turn for a house command."""
    hookio.atomic_write_json(hookio.state_dir() / HOUSE_NAME, {"skin": word})
    ask = HOUSE_ASK_OFF if word == HOUSE_OFF else HOUSE_ASK.format(skin=word)
    hookio.emit_context(HOOK_EVENT, HOUSE_LINE.format(skin=word) + "\n" + ask)
    telemetry.append(
        score.Decision(
            skin=word, tier=score.tier_of(word, rules), score=TYPED_SCORE, source=HOUSE_SOURCE
        ),
        session_id,
    )


def _router_line(skin: str, tier: str, source: str, value: float = TYPED_SCORE) -> str:
    """The line every injection opens with, so a contract turn reads exactly like a routed one."""
    return f"[router] skin={skin} score={value:.2f} tier={tier} source={source}"


def _edit_turn(message: str, rules: dict[str, Any], session_id: str) -> None:
    """Answer a numbered edit: what changed, the contract as it now reads, and what to say next."""
    summary = ledger.summary() or {}
    tier = score.tier_of(EDIT_SKIN, rules)
    heading = LEDGER_HEADING.format(
        contract_id=summary.get("contract_id") or ledger.next_id(),
        title=str(summary.get("title") or "").strip() or skins.NO_CONTRACT_TITLE,
    )
    lines = [_router_line(EDIT_SKIN, tier, EDIT_SOURCE), message, heading]
    rendered = ledger.view()
    if rendered.strip():
        lines.append(rendered.strip("\n"))
    lines.append(EDIT_ASK)

    hookio.emit_context(HOOK_EVENT, hookio.clip_bytes("\n".join(lines), hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES))
    telemetry.append(
        score.Decision(skin=EDIT_SKIN, tier=tier, score=TYPED_SCORE, source=EDIT_SOURCE),
        session_id,
    )


def _catch_up_turn(session_id: str) -> None:
    """Answer the question a cleared window asks: which contract, how it stands, what is still owed.

    The cat is the sleepy one because the session has just woken up, and the two data lines come
    from the ledger itself, so the only thing the model writes is the line about what is left.
    """
    data = ledger.lite_lines()
    beside = (data + [""] * (LITE_LINES - 1 - len(data)))[: LITE_LINES - 1] + [LITE_SLOT]
    text = "\n".join(
        [
            _router_line(LITE_SKIN, LITE_TIER, LITE_SOURCE),
            LITE_ASK,
            skins.with_cat(beside, skins.CAT_SLEEPY),
            skins.NEVER_MENTION,
        ]
    )

    hookio.emit_context(HOOK_EVENT, hookio.clip_bytes(text, hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES))
    telemetry.append(
        score.Decision(skin=LITE_SKIN, tier=LITE_TIER, score=TYPED_SCORE, source=LITE_SOURCE),
        session_id,
    )


def _house_skin(rules: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
    """The member's standing floor, when they set one and the rules still know that skin."""
    data = hookio.read_json(hookio.state_dir() / HOUSE_NAME, {})
    skin = data.get("skin") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(skin, str) or not skin or skin == HOUSE_OFF:
        return None
    known = rules.get("skins")
    if not isinstance(known, dict) or skin not in known:
        hookio.log(f"router: the house floor names {skin}, which is not one of the skins; ignoring it")
        return None
    return skin


def _decide(
    prompt: str,
    rules: dict[str, Any],
    state: signals.State,
    house_skin: str | None,
    transcript_path: str | None = None,
) -> score.Decision | None:
    """The skin for this prompt, or ``None`` when the router should stay out of the way.

    The explicit token wins outright. After that the rules decide who reads the prompt: with the
    classifier switched on it is Haiku, reading the last few turns as well as this one because a
    short prompt means whatever the conversation was about; a Haiku that cannot answer hands the
    turn straight back to the scorer, which is why that decision carries its own source word.
    """
    decision = score.explicit_decision(prompt, rules)
    if decision is not None:
        return decision

    tokens = prompt.split()
    if len(tokens) == 1 and tokens[0].startswith("/"):
        # why: a bare slash command such as /clear or /config belongs to Claude Code, not to us
        return None

    if rules.get("classifier") == HAIKU_CLASSIFIER:
        recent = transcript.recent_turns(transcript_path, transcript.DEFAULT_TURNS)
        decision = haiku.classify(prompt, recent, rules)
        if decision is not None:
            return decision
        decision = score.decide(prompt, state, rules, house_skin)
        decision.source = decision.source + HAIKU_FAILED
        return decision

    return score.decide(prompt, state, rules, house_skin)


def main() -> None:
    """Read the payload, decide, inject once. One stdout write on the happy path, none otherwise."""
    try:
        if hookio.is_child_process():
            sys.exit(0)

        data = hookio.read_hook_input()
        prompt = str(data.get("prompt") or "").strip()
        session_id = str(data.get("session_id") or "")
        if not prompt:
            sys.exit(0)

        try:
            rules = rules_lib.load_rules()
        except rules_lib.RulesError as exc:
            hookio.log(f"router: {exc}; carrying on with the fallback rules")
            rules = rules_lib.FALLBACK_RULES

        house_word = _house_command(prompt, rules)
        if house_word is not None:
            _set_house(house_word, rules, session_id)
            sys.exit(0)

        edited = ledger.apply_edit(prompt)
        if edited is not None:
            _edit_turn(edited, rules, session_id)
            sys.exit(0)

        state = signals.read_state()
        lexicons = rules.get("lexicons")
        if (
            signals.go_word(prompt, state, lexicons) == signals.HIT
            and state.ledger_on_disk
            and not state.ledger_approved
        ):
            ledger.approve(session_id)
            # why: the contract is agreed as of this turn, and the skin for this turn reads that
            state = signals.read_state()

        if state.ledger_on_disk and CATCH_UP.fullmatch(prompt.lower()):
            _catch_up_turn(session_id)
            sys.exit(0)

        # why: the harness sends a path or nothing; anything else is read as nothing rather than
        # risking the turn on a field we did not write
        where = str(data.get("transcript_path") or "").strip() or None
        decision = _decide(prompt, rules, state, _house_skin(rules), where)
        if decision is None:
            sys.exit(0)

        text = hookio.clip_bytes(
            skins.render_injection(
                decision,
                ledger_view=ledger.view(),
                activity=telemetry.activity(),
                ledger_summary=ledger.summary(),
                next_contract_id=ledger.next_id(),
            ),
            hookio.ROUTER_MAX_BYTES,
        )
        hookio.emit_context(HOOK_EVENT, text)
        telemetry.append(decision, session_id)
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — the never-crash law: the member's prompt goes through regardless
        hookio.log("router: " + traceback.format_exc())
        sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/__init__.py
================================================
"""Shared library for the prompt-router hooks."""



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/font.py
================================================
"""Block-letter rendering from a vendored FIGlet font, stdlib only.

Reads `fonts/ansi-shadow.flf` and turns a word into the rows of block letters the
skins print as their header. No smushing and no kerning: glyphs are concatenated
as the font draws them, so each glyph's own trailing spaces are the letter gap.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

FONT_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fonts" / "ansi-shadow.flf"

SIGNATURE = "flf2a"  # why: the FIGfont v2 magic; the character straight after it is the font's hardblank
FIRST_CODEPOINT = 32  # why: a .flf stores its required glyphs in ASCII order starting at space (32)
LAST_CODEPOINT = 126  # why: the required run ends at tilde (126); anything beyond needs code tags we do not use
HEADER_FIELDS = 6  # why: signature+hardblank, height, baseline, max_length, old_layout, comment_lines are the fields we read


class FontError(ValueError):
    """The font file is missing, truncated, or not a FIGfont v2 file."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Font:
    """One parsed FIGfont: its measurements plus a glyph table keyed by character."""

    height: int
    baseline: int
    max_length: int
    old_layout: int
    comment_lines: int
    hardblank: str
    comment: str
    glyphs: dict[str, list[str]]

    def glyph(self, char: str) -> list[str]:
        """The rows for `char`, falling back to the space glyph for anything unknown."""
        rows = self.glyphs.get(char)
        if rows is not None:
            return rows
        blank = self.glyphs.get(" ")
        if blank is not None:
            return blank
        return [""] * self.height


_CACHE: dict[Path, Font] = {}  # why: hooks render a header on every prompt; parsing the file once per process keeps that cheap


def _parse_header(line: str) -> tuple[str, int, int, int, int, int]:
    """Split the first line into hardblank, height, baseline, max_length, old_layout, comment_lines."""
    parts = line.split()
    if len(parts) < HEADER_FIELDS or not parts[0].startswith(SIGNATURE):
        raise FontError(f"header: not a FIGfont v2 header line: {line[:40]!r}")
    hardblank = parts[0][len(SIGNATURE):]
    if len(hardblank) != 1:
        raise FontError(f"header: expected one hardblank character after {SIGNATURE!r}, got {hardblank!r}")
    try:
        height, baseline, max_length, old_layout, comment_lines = (int(p) for p in parts[1:HEADER_FIELDS])
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise FontError(f"header: non-numeric measurement in {line[:40]!r}") from exc
    if height < 1:
        raise FontError(f"header: height must be at least 1, got {height}")
    if comment_lines < 0:
        raise FontError(f"header: comment_lines must not be negative, got {comment_lines}")
    return hardblank, height, baseline, max_length, old_layout, comment_lines


def _strip_endmark(row: str) -> str:
    """Drop the row's end-mark characters; the last character of the row is the mark."""
    if not row:
        return row
    mark = row[-1]
    return row.rstrip(mark)


def load_font(path: Path = FONT_PATH) -> Font:
    """Parse a .flf file into a Font. Results are cached per resolved path."""
    key = Path(path).resolve()
    cached = _CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    try:
        # newline=None: a font file checked out with CRLF still splits into clean rows.
        with key.open(encoding="utf-8", newline=None) as handle:
            lines = handle.read().split("\n")
    except OSError as exc:
        raise FontError(f"path: cannot read font file {key}") from exc
    hardblank, height, baseline, max_length, old_layout, comment_lines = _parse_header(lines[0])
    body = lines[1 + comment_lines:]
    glyphs: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for index, codepoint in enumerate(range(FIRST_CODEPOINT, LAST_CODEPOINT + 1)):
        start = index * height
        rows = body[start:start + height]
        if len(rows) < height:
            raise FontError(f"glyphs: font ends inside the glyph for codepoint {codepoint}")
        glyphs[chr(codepoint)] = [_strip_endmark(row).replace(hardblank, " ") for row in rows]
    font = Font(
        height=height,
        baseline=baseline,
        max_length=max_length,
        old_layout=old_layout,
        comment_lines=comment_lines,
        hardblank=hardblank,
        comment="\n".join(lines[1:1 + comment_lines]),
        glyphs=glyphs,
    )
    _CACHE[key] = font
    return font


def render(word: str, font: Font | None = None) -> list[str]:
    """Render `word` as block-letter rows: uppercased, glyphs concatenated, each row right-trimmed.

    Rows that end up empty at the bottom are dropped, so a word of capitals comes
    back as the six drawn rows rather than the font's seven-row cell.
    """
    active = font if font is not None else load_font()
    cells = [active.glyph(char) for char in word.upper()]
    rows = ["".join(cell[index] for cell in cells).rstrip() for index in range(active.height)]
    while rows and not rows[-1]:
        rows.pop()
    return rows


def render_block(word: str, font: Font | None = None) -> str:
    """`render` joined with newlines: the header block a skin prints verbatim."""
    return "\n".join(render(word, font))



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/haiku.py
================================================
"""The optional model classifier: one Haiku call in place of the scored signals.

``rules.json`` ships ``"classifier": "rules"``, so nothing here runs unless the member switches it
on. When it is on, the router asks a headless ``claude --print --model haiku`` which skin fits the
prompt, and takes the answer only when it names a skin the repo actually has. A missing binary, a
timeout, an unreadable answer: every one of them returns ``None``, and the router falls back to the
deterministic scorer.

Two hard rules:

- **Never call this from a Stop hook.** A Stop hook runs after the model answered, so a Claude call
  inside it can produce another Stop. The spawn belongs to ``UserPromptSubmit`` only, before there
  is a reply to gate.
- **Never call it from a child of our own hooks.** ``PROMPT_ROUTER_CHILD`` is set in the spawned
  environment and checked again on the way in, so the classifier's own Claude session stands its
  hooks down and cannot classify its way into a loop.

The child environment drops ``CLAUDECODE`` (Claude Code sets it in hook subprocesses, and the child
must not read itself as a hook run) and both API-key variables, so the nested call bills the
member's subscription the way their own session does rather than a key that happens to be set.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
from typing import Any

from lib import hookio, skins
from lib.score import Decision, tier_of

CLASSIFIER_TIMEOUT_S = 8  # why: a hook must answer inside its 10 s budget
CLAUDE_BINARY = "claude"  # why: the CLI's name on PATH; shutil.which resolves claude.cmd on Windows
MODEL_ALIAS = "haiku"  # why: the alias, never a dated id, so the model can move without an edit here
SOURCE = "haiku"  # why: the word the router line prints for a decision that came from the model
SETTING_SOURCES = "user"  # why: the member's own login and settings, none of the project's hooks
MAX_TURNS = "1"  # why: one answer, no tool loop, so the call cannot outrun the timeout
NO_TOOLS = ""  # why: an empty tool list; the classifier reads a sentence, it never touches the repo
SCRUBBED_ENV = (
    "CLAUDECODE",
    "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
    "ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN",
)  # why: the first makes the child think it is a hook run, the other two would bill an API key
MIN_CONFIDENCE = 0.0  # why: the JSON contract's floor; anything under it is a misread, not a number
MAX_CONFIDENCE = 1.0  # why: the JSON contract's ceiling, same reason
SCORE_DIGITS = 2  # why: the router line prints two decimals, so the stored score carries two

# why: the model is asked for bare JSON and sometimes wraps it in a fence anyway; this takes the
# body out of ```json ... ``` or ``` ... ``` and leaves unfenced text untouched.
_FENCE = re.compile(r"\A```[A-Za-z0-9_+-]*[ \t]*\r?\n?(?P<body>.*?)\r?\n?```\Z", re.DOTALL)

# why: eight worked examples, one per skin, written fresh so no demo line teaches the classifier
# the demo's own answers. Each is (prompt, skin, confidence, reason).
EXAMPLES: tuple[tuple[str, str, float, str], ...] = (
    (
        "is it worth paying for the bigger runner or living with the slow build",
        "bluf",
        0.86,
        "one decision between two options",
    ),
    ("how far along is the signup flow", "sitrep", 0.8, "asks where the work stands"),
    (
        "the welcome email has to go out on day one, the trial ends at 14 days, "
        "keep the old invite links working, and it should be live by tuesday",
        "contract",
        0.88,
        "four requirements to agree before any building",
    ),
