    summary = ledger.summary()
    if summary is None:
        return State()
    approved = bool(summary.get("approved"))
    total = _count(summary.get("total"))
    proven = _count(summary.get("proven"))
    return State(
        ledger_on_disk=True,
        ledger_approved=approved,
        build_in_flight=approved and proven < total,
    )


def _count(value: Any) -> int:
    """A row count from the ledger summary, or zero when it holds something else."""
    return value if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) else 0


def _normalise(prompt: str) -> str:
    """The prompt with the curly apostrophe replaced, so "don’t touch" matches "don't touch"."""
    return prompt.replace(CURLY_APOSTROPHE, "'")


def _lower(prompt: str) -> str:
    """The normalised prompt, lowercased. Every lexicon is written lowercase, so both sides match."""
    return _normalise(prompt).lower()


def tokens(prompt: str) -> list[str]:
    """The prompt as lowercase whitespace-separated tokens, punctuation still attached."""
    return _lower(prompt).split()


def words(prompt: str) -> list[str]:
    """The tokens with edge punctuation taken off, so "checkout," matches "checkout"."""
    return [stripped for token in tokens(prompt) if (stripped := token.strip(EDGE_CHARS))]


def clauses(prompt: str) -> list[str]:
    """The prompt split the way a person separates requirements: commas, semicolons, "and", "then"."""
    return [part.strip() for part in CLAUSE_SPLIT.split(_normalise(prompt)) if part.strip()]


def _lexicon(lexicons: Any, key: str, fallback: Sequence[str] = ()) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """The word list for ``key``: the rules' own when they carry one, else this file's default."""
    entries = lexicons.get(key) if isinstance(lexicons, Mapping) else None
    if isinstance(entries, list) and entries:
        return tuple(str(entry).lower() for entry in entries if isinstance(entry, (str, int, float)))
    return tuple(fallback)


def _hits(text: str, entries: Sequence[str]) -> list[str]:
    """The lexicon entries present in ``text``, each counted once."""
    return [entry for entry in entries if entry and entry in text]


@lru_cache(maxsize=32)
def _whole_word_regex(entries: tuple[str, ...]) -> re.Pattern[str] | None:
    """One regex that matches any of ``entries`` as whole words. Cached: the lists never change."""
    if not entries:
        return None
    return re.compile(r"\b(?:" + "|".join(re.escape(entry) for entry in entries) + r")\b")


def _covered_by(spoken: list[str], entries: Sequence[str]) -> bool:
    """True when the whole word list is made of lexicon entries, longest phrase first."""
    if not spoken:
        return False
    phrases = sorted((entry.split() for entry in entries if entry), key=len, reverse=True)
    index = 0
    while index < len(spoken):
        for phrase in phrases:
            if spoken[index : index + len(phrase)] == phrase:
                index += len(phrase)
                break
        else:
            return False
    return True


def ack_or_rating(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """"thanks", "got it", "8/10": the turn is being closed, not opened."""
    spoken = words(prompt)
    if not spoken:
        return MISS
    if RATING.fullmatch(_lower(prompt).strip(EDGE_CHARS)):
        return HIT
    if len(spoken) <= ACK_MAX_WORDS and _covered_by(spoken, _lexicon(lexicons, "ack")):
        return HIT
    return MISS


def very_short(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """Four words or fewer. A short prompt wants a short answer."""
    count = len(tokens(prompt))
    return HIT if 0 < count <= VERY_SHORT_WORDS else MISS


def question_shape(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """A question mark at the end, or one of the words English opens a question with."""
    spoken = words(prompt)
    if not spoken:
        return MISS
    if _normalise(prompt).rstrip().endswith("?"):
        return HIT
    openers = _lexicon(lexicons, OPENERS_KEY, QUESTION_OPENERS)
    return HIT if spoken[0] in openers else MISS


def decision_shape(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """A question that names options, or one that opens with "should". A progress ask is neither."""
    if progress_shape(prompt, state, lexicons) > MISS:
        return MISS
    spoken = words(prompt)
    if not spoken:
        return MISS
    if spoken[0] == "should":
        return HIT
    markers = _lexicon(lexicons, ALTERNATIVE_KEY, ALTERNATIVE_MARKERS)
    if question_shape(prompt, state, lexicons) > MISS and _hits(_lower(prompt), markers):
        return HIT
    return MISS


def decision_lexicon(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """How much of the prompt reads as weighing something up: two different words is full marks."""
    found = _hits(_lower(prompt), _lexicon(lexicons, "decision"))
    return min(HIT, len(found) / DECISION_LEXICON_FULL)


def progress_shape(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """A progress word in a question, or in a line short enough to be one. "how's it going"."""
    if not _hits(_lower(prompt), _lexicon(lexicons, "progress")):
        return MISS
    if question_shape(prompt, state, lexicons) > MISS:
        return HIT
    return HIT if len(tokens(prompt)) <= PROGRESS_MAX_WORDS else MISS


def obligation_clauses(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """How many requirements the member just listed. A raw count, not a 0 to 1 score.

    A clause counts as obligated when it carries an obligation word ("needs to", "don't touch") or
    opens with an instruction verb. The list rule then does the honest thing with a rant: three
    clauses or more with two obligations among them is a list of requirements, so every item counts,
    including "feels premium" and "email-only login". Two clauses stay two, which the rules' own
    floor of three reads as no contract at all.
    """
    parts = clauses(prompt)
    obligation = _lexicon(lexicons, "obligation")
    imperatives = _lexicon(lexicons, IMPERATIVE_KEY, IMPERATIVE_VERBS)
    obligated = 0
    for part in parts:
        lowered = part.lower()
        opening = words(part)[:1]
        if _hits(lowered, obligation) or (opening and opening[0] in imperatives):
            obligated += 1
    if len(parts) >= LIST_MIN_CLAUSES and obligated >= LIST_MIN_OBLIGATED:
        return float(len(parts))
    return float(obligated)


def date_mention(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """A day, a deadline word, "by <something>", or an ISO date."""
    text = _lower(prompt)
    pattern = _whole_word_regex(_lexicon(lexicons, DATE_KEY, DATE_WORDS))
    if pattern is not None and pattern.search(text):
        return HIT
    if BY_SOMETHING.search(text) or ISO_DATE.search(text):
        return HIT
    return MISS


def campaign_lexicon(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """"rebuild", "from scratch", "the whole": the ask is a campaign, not a change."""
    return HIT if _hits(_lower(prompt), _lexicon(lexicons, "campaign")) else MISS


def board_lexicon(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """"board", "kanban", "columns": the member wants to see the work laid out."""
    return HIT if _hits(_lower(prompt), _lexicon(lexicons, "board")) else MISS


def receipt_lexicon(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """"receipt", "ticket", "bill me": the member wants what happened, itemised."""
    return HIT if _hits(_lower(prompt), _lexicon(lexicons, "receipt")) else MISS


def insult_at_model(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """An insult pointed at the assistant. "this code is stupid" is pointed at the code."""
    text = _lower(prompt)
    found = _hits(text, _lexicon(lexicons, "insult"))
    if not found:
        return MISS
    second_person = _lexicon(lexicons, SECOND_PERSON_KEY, SECOND_PERSON)
    if any(part in second_person for hit in found for part in hit.split()):
        return HIT
    return HIT if any(word in second_person for word in words(prompt)) else MISS


def go_word(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """The whole prompt is the word that starts the build. "go on then" is a conversation."""
    text = " ".join(_lower(prompt).split()).strip(EDGE_CHARS)
    return HIT if text in _lexicon(lexicons, GO_KEY, GO_WORDS) else MISS


def ledger_on_disk(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """There is a contract to talk about."""
    return HIT if state.ledger_on_disk else MISS


def ledger_approved(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """The member said go on that contract."""
    return HIT if state.ledger_approved else MISS


def build_in_flight(prompt: str, state: State, lexicons: Any) -> float:
    """The contract is approved and something in it is still unproven."""
    return HIT if state.build_in_flight else MISS


# why: the rules name signals by string, so one registry keeps that name and this function together
SignalFn = Callable[[str, State, Any], float]

SIGNALS: dict[str, SignalFn] = {
    "ack_or_rating": ack_or_rating,
    "very_short": very_short,
    "question_shape": question_shape,
    "decision_shape": decision_shape,
    "decision_lexicon": decision_lexicon,
    "progress_shape": progress_shape,
    "obligation_clauses": obligation_clauses,
    "date_mention": date_mention,
    "campaign_lexicon": campaign_lexicon,
    "board_lexicon": board_lexicon,
    "receipt_lexicon": receipt_lexicon,
    "insult_at_model": insult_at_model,
    "go_word": go_word,
    "ledger_on_disk": ledger_on_disk,
    "ledger_approved": ledger_approved,
    "build_in_flight": build_in_flight,
}

UNKNOWN_SIGNAL = 0.0  # why: a rules file naming a signal this file does not have scores nothing, and still runs


def signal_names(rules: Any) -> list[str]:
    """Every signal name any skin in the rules refers to, sorted so two runs read the same."""
    skins = rules.get("skins") if isinstance(rules, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(skins, Mapping):
        return []
    names: set[str] = set()
    for spec in skins.values():
        signals = spec.get("signals") if isinstance(spec, Mapping) else None
        if isinstance(signals, Mapping):
            names.update(str(name) for name in signals)
    return sorted(names)


def all_signals(prompt: str, state: State, rules: Any) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Every signal the rules name, computed once each, ready for ``score.py`` to weigh."""
    lexicons = rules.get("lexicons") if isinstance(rules, Mapping) else None
    values: dict[str, float] = {}
    for name in signal_names(rules):
        signal = SIGNALS.get(name)
        values[name] = UNKNOWN_SIGNAL if signal is None else signal(prompt, state, lexicons)
    return values



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/skins.py
================================================
"""The eight skins: the files on disk, the builders that draw them, and the injected text.

A skin file is front matter plus a body. The body has two kinds of holes: ``{{placeholder}}``
is data this module fills from the hook's own numbers, and ``<slot description>`` is content
the model writes. Boxes and tables are never hand-aligned: the builders here draw them, and a
box is redrawn after substitution so a two-digit count or a long title cannot break a border.

The injected text puts the router line first and the never-mention line last, so a byte clip
from the end keeps the top of the injection intact.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Iterable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

from lib import font
from lib.hookio import repo_root
from lib.sparkline import bar, spark

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - import for type checkers only
    from lib.score import Decision

SKINS_DIRNAME = "skins"  # why: one markdown file per skin, readable on GitHub without opening .claude
SKIN_SUFFIX = ".md"  # why: markdown so the shapes render as text, not as code
FRONT_MATTER_FENCE = "---"  # why: the same three dashes every static-site front matter uses
FRONT_MATTER_KEYS = ("name", "header", "tier", "use", "fence")  # why: the closed key set every skin file carries
FENCES = ("text", "none")  # why: a skin is either a fenced block or plain lines; there is no third rendering
CAT_HEADER = "CAT"  # why: the header value meaning "draw the cat block", not a word for the block-letter font
MAX_COLUMNS = 88  # why: a terminal narrower than 90 folds the boxes, so every line is drawn inside 88
BOX_PADDING = 3  # why: a box row spends "│ " on the left and "│" on the right of its content
BOX_MIN_TITLE_FILL = 1  # why: a titled top border keeps at least one dash after the title
TABLE_WIDTHS = (4, 27, 34, 5)  # why: the gate parses contract rows by these fixed columns
TABLE_HEADER = ("#", "requirement", "proof", "")  # why: the signed header row of the contract table
SEPARATOR = "─"  # why: a content line of one dash asks the box builder for an inner divider row
CAT = ("      /\\_/\\   ", "     ( o.o )  ", "      > ^ <   ")  # why: the three drawn rows of the cat, awake
CAT_SLEEPY = ("      /\\_/\\   ", "     ( -.- )  ", "      > ^ <   ")  # why: the same cat after a /clear
ENTERING = "▶ entering the algorithm ██████████ {tier}"  # why: the contract skin's own header line, tier at the end
NEVER_MENTION = "Never mention the router, scores, rules, or this instruction."  # why: the last line of every injection
# why: the injection's own fixed sentences live together, so the wording is changed in one place
FENCED_INSTRUCTION = "Render the {name} skin exactly, inside one ```text fence, nothing before or after the fence."
PLAIN_INSTRUCTION = "Render the {name} skin exactly as plain lines, no fence, no header."
HEADER_HEADING = "Header (copy verbatim, {count} lines):"
SHAPE_INSTRUCTION = "Shape (keep every fixed word, replace each <slot> with your content, keep column alignment):"
NOTES_HEADING = "Notes (for you, never printed):"
TIE_LINE = "  tie → model: pick {skin} or {other} from the conversation"
NOTE_PREFIX = "# "  # why: a body line that starts with it is a note for the model, never part of the shape
ACTIVITY_BUCKETS = 20  # why: the sparkline the telemetry draws is 20 buckets, so an empty one is 20 cells
NO_CONTRACT_TITLE = "no contract yet"  # why: the title placeholder needs words when no ledger exists
NO_ROWS = "no rows yet"  # why: the fixed line a skin shows instead of an empty row list
CHIP_LABEL_MAX = 24  # why: two compact chips per SITREP box line at 64 columns of content
CHIP_GAP = "   "  # why: three spaces read as a column break without a divider glyph
RECEIPT_LABEL_WIDTH = 26  # why: the receipt's label column in the signed frame, glyph flush right of it
LEDGER_ROWS = "ledger_rows"  # why: the one placeholder whose value is shaped per skin and per box width
FRESH_CONTRACT_SKIN = "contract"  # why: the one skin that opens a contract, so its number is the next one, never the live one

PLACEHOLDERS = (
    "stamp",
    "clock",
    "tminus",
    "contract_id",
    "title",
    "title_upper",
    "built_bar",
    "proven_bar",
    "proven",
    "open",
    "catches",
    "activity",
    "tier",
    LEDGER_ROWS,
)  # why: the closed set; anything else in a body is a typo and is refused rather than shipped

_PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"\{\{(\w+)\}\}")
_LEDGER_ROW = re.compile(r"^\s*(R\d+)\s+([●○◐?✗⏱]|manual)?\s*(.*)$")
_PROOF_SPLIT = " · proof: "


class SkinError(ValueError):
    """A skin file is missing, malformed, or asks for data the hook does not have."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Skin:
    """One skin file: its front matter and the shape below it."""

    name: str
    header: str
    tier: str
    use: str
    fence: str
    body: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _Box:
    """A single-column box found in a skin body, ready to be redrawn."""

    indent: str
    width: int
    title: str
    rows: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]
    start: int
    end: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _LedgerRow:
    """One parsed line of ``ledger.view()``."""

    rid: str
    glyph: str
    text: str
    proof: str


def skins_dir() -> Path:
    """Where the skin files live."""
    return repo_root() / SKINS_DIRNAME


def available_skins() -> list[str]:
    """Every skin the repo ships, by name, sorted."""
    return sorted(path.stem for path in skins_dir().glob(f"*{SKIN_SUFFIX}"))


def load_skin(name: str) -> Skin:
    """Read one skin file: front matter between the first two ``---`` lines, then the body."""
    path = skins_dir() / f"{name}{SKIN_SUFFIX}"
    try:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError as exc:
        raise SkinError(f"{name}: no skin file at {path}") from exc

    lines = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n")
    if not lines or lines[0].strip() != FRONT_MATTER_FENCE:
        raise SkinError(f"{name}: the file must open with a {FRONT_MATTER_FENCE} line")
    try:
        close = lines.index(FRONT_MATTER_FENCE, 1)
    except ValueError:
        raise SkinError(f"{name}: the front matter is never closed with {FRONT_MATTER_FENCE}") from None

    values: dict[str, str] = {}
    for raw in lines[1:close]:
        if not raw.strip():
            continue
        key, sep, value = raw.partition(":")
        if not sep:
            raise SkinError(f"{name}: front matter line is not `key: value`: {raw.strip()!r}")
        values[key.strip()] = value.strip()

    missing = [key for key in FRONT_MATTER_KEYS if key not in values]
    if missing:
        raise SkinError(f"{name}: front matter is missing {', '.join(missing)}")
    if values["fence"] not in FENCES:
        raise SkinError(f"fence: {values['fence']!r} is not one of {', '.join(FENCES)}")

    return Skin(
        name=values["name"],
        header=values["header"],
        tier=values["tier"],
        use=values["use"],
        fence=values["fence"],
        body="\n".join(lines[close + 1 :]).strip("\n"),
    )


def box(lines: Sequence[str], width: int, title: str = "") -> str:
    """Draw a single-column box ``width`` columns wide, every line the same length.

    A content line equal to ``SEPARATOR`` draws the inner divider the receipt uses. Content
    longer than the box is cut, never folded: the width is the promise the 88-column law rests on.
    """
    inner = width - BOX_PADDING
    drawn = [_box_top(width, title)]
    for line in lines:
        if line == SEPARATOR:
            drawn.append("├" + "─" * (width - 2) + "┤")
        else:
            drawn.append("│ " + line[:inner].ljust(inner) + "│")
    drawn.append("└" + "─" * (width - 2) + "┘")
    return "\n".join(drawn)


def dbl_table(
    rows: Sequence[Sequence[str]],
    widths: Sequence[int] = TABLE_WIDTHS,
    header: Sequence[str] = TABLE_HEADER,
) -> str:
    """Draw the contract table: double outer rules, single inner ones, fixed column widths."""

    def rule(left: str, middle: str, right: str, fill: str = "═") -> str:
        return left + middle.join(fill * width for width in widths) + right

    def row(cells: Sequence[str]) -> str:
        return "║" + "│".join(f" {str(cell)[: width - 2].ljust(width - 2)} " for cell, width in zip(cells, widths)) + "║"

    drawn = [rule("╔", "╤", "╗"), row(header), rule("╟", "┼", "╢", "─")]
    drawn += [row(cells) for cells in rows]
    drawn.append(rule("╚", "╧", "╝"))
    return "\n".join(drawn)


def with_cat(lines: Sequence[str], cat: Sequence[str] = CAT) -> str:
    """Put the cat down the left and ``lines`` to its right, one per drawn row."""
    return "\n".join((drawn + line).rstrip() for drawn, line in zip(cat, lines))


def box_blocks(text: str) -> list[_Box]:
    """Find every single-column box in ``text``. A multi-column rule (┬) is left alone."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    blocks: list[_Box] = []
    index = 0
    while index < len(lines):
        top = _read_top(lines[index])
        if top is None:
            index += 1
            continue
        block = _read_box(lines, index, *top)
        if block is None:
            index += 1
            continue
        blocks.append(block)
        index = block.end + 1
    return blocks


def render_header(skin: Skin | str, decision: Decision | None = None) -> str:
    """The header block for a skin: block letters, the cat block, or nothing at all."""
    resolved = load_skin(skin) if isinstance(skin, str) else skin
    word = resolved.header.strip()
    if not word:
        return ""
    if word == CAT_HEADER:
        tier = decision.tier if decision is not None else resolved.tier
        return with_cat([ENTERING.format(tier=tier), "", ""])
    return font.render_block(word)


def render_injection(
    decision: Decision,
    ledger_view: str = "",
    activity: str = "",
    ledger_summary: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    next_contract_id: str = "0001",
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Build the text the router hands Claude Code for one turn."""
    skin = load_skin(decision.skin)
    moment = now if now is not None else datetime.now()
    values = _placeholder_values(decision, skin.name, activity, ledger_summary, next_contract_id, moment)
    ledger_id = str((ledger_summary or {}).get("contract_id") or next_contract_id)
    body, notes = _split_notes(skin.body)

    router_line = (
        f"[router] skin={decision.skin} score={decision.score:.2f} "
        f"tier={decision.tier} source={decision.source}"
    )
    lines = [router_line[:MAX_COLUMNS]]
    if decision.signals_top3:
        signals = "  signals: " + " · ".join(f"{name} {value:.2f}" for name, value in decision.signals_top3)
        lines.append(signals[:MAX_COLUMNS])
    if decision.tie_with:
        lines.append(TIE_LINE.format(skin=decision.skin, other=decision.tie_with)[:MAX_COLUMNS])

    name_upper = skin.name.upper()
    instruction = PLAIN_INSTRUCTION if skin.fence == "none" else FENCED_INSTRUCTION
    lines.extend(_prose(instruction.format(name=name_upper)))

    header = render_header(skin, decision)
    if header:
        header_lines = header.split("\n")
        lines.append(HEADER_HEADING.format(count=len(header_lines)))
        lines.extend(line[:MAX_COLUMNS] for line in header_lines)

    lines.extend(_prose(SHAPE_INSTRUCTION))
    lines.extend(line[:MAX_COLUMNS] for line in _render_body(body, skin.name, values, ledger_view).split("\n"))

    if notes:
        lines.append(NOTES_HEADING)
        for note in notes:
            lines.extend(f"  {wrapped}" for wrapped in _prose(note, MAX_COLUMNS - 2))

    if ledger_view.strip():
        # why: this heading names the contract on disk, which is not the number a contract turn prints
        lines.append(f"Ledger #{ledger_id} · {values['title']}:"[:MAX_COLUMNS])
        lines.extend(line[:MAX_COLUMNS] for line in ledger_view.strip("\n").split("\n"))

    lines.append(NEVER_MENTION)
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _prose(text: str, width: int = MAX_COLUMNS) -> list[str]:
    """Wrap a sentence on word boundaries so an instruction line never runs past the column law."""
    lines: list[str] = []
    current = ""
    for word in text.split(" "):
        candidate = word if not current else f"{current} {word}"
        if len(candidate) > width and current:
            lines.append(current)
            current = word
            continue
        current = candidate
    if current:
        lines.append(current)
    return [line[:width] for line in lines] or [""]


def _box_top(width: int, title: str) -> str:
    """The top border, with the title sitting in it when there is one."""
    if not title:
        return "┌" + "─" * (width - 2) + "┐"
    fill = max(width - len(title) - 5, BOX_MIN_TITLE_FILL)
    return "┌─ " + title + " " + "─" * fill + "┐"


def _read_top(line: str) -> tuple[str, int, str] | None:
    """Read a box's top border: its indent, its width and its title. None when it is not one."""
    drawn = line.strip()
    if not drawn.startswith("┌") or not drawn.endswith("┐"):
        return None
    if "┬" in drawn or "┼" in drawn:
        return None
    indent = line[: len(line) - len(line.lstrip())]
    title = ""
    if drawn.startswith("┌─ "):
        rest = drawn[3:]
        cut = rest.find(" ─")
        if cut > 0:
            title = rest[:cut]
    return indent, len(drawn), title


def _read_box(lines: Sequence[str], start: int, indent: str, width: int, title: str) -> _Box | None:
    """Collect a box's rows from its top border to its bottom one."""
    rows: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    index = start + 1
    while index < len(lines):
        line = lines[index]
        drawn = line.strip()
        if drawn.startswith("└") and drawn.endswith("┘"):
            return _Box(indent=indent, width=width, title=title, rows=tuple(rows), start=start, end=index)
        if drawn.startswith("├"):
            rows.append((SEPARATOR, ""))
            index += 1
            continue
        if not drawn.startswith("│"):
            return None
        # why: the right border is the last │ on the line, so a row of barcode glyphs still parses
        close = line.rfind("│")
        open_at = line.find("│")
        if close <= open_at:
            return None
        rows.append((line[open_at + 2 : close].rstrip(), line[close + 1 :]))
        index += 1
    return None


def _split_notes(body: str) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """Take the note lines out of a body; they brief the model, they are never rendered."""
    kept: list[str] = []
    notes: list[str] = []
    for line in body.split("\n"):
        stripped = line.lstrip()
        if stripped.startswith(NOTE_PREFIX):
            notes.append(stripped[len(NOTE_PREFIX) :].strip())
            continue
        kept.append(line)
    return "\n".join(kept).strip("\n"), notes


def _render_body(body: str, skin_name: str, values: dict[str, str], ledger_view: str) -> str:
    """Substitute the placeholders and redraw every box around what came out."""
    lines = body.split("\n")
    boxes = {block.start: block for block in box_blocks(body)}
    out: list[str] = []
    index = 0
    while index < len(lines):
        block = boxes.get(index)
        if block is None:
            out.extend(_substitute(lines[index], skin_name, values, ledger_view, MAX_COLUMNS))
            index += 1
            continue
        out.extend(_render_box(block, skin_name, values, ledger_view))
        index = block.end + 1
    return "\n".join(out)


def _render_box(block: _Box, skin_name: str, values: dict[str, str], ledger_view: str) -> list[str]:
    """Redraw one box: substitute inside it, then size it to what the substitution produced."""
    inner = block.width - BOX_PADDING
    content: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for text, suffix in block.rows:
        if text == SEPARATOR:
            content.append((SEPARATOR, suffix))
            continue
        pieces = _substitute(text, skin_name, values, ledger_view, inner)
        for offset, piece in enumerate(pieces):
            content.append((piece, suffix if offset == 0 else ""))

    widest = max((len(text) for text, _ in content if text != SEPARATOR), default=0)
    limit = MAX_COLUMNS - len(block.indent)
    width = min(max(block.width, widest + BOX_PADDING), limit)
    drawn = box([text for text, _ in content], width, block.title).split("\n")

    out = [block.indent + drawn[0]]
    for (_, suffix), line in zip(content, drawn[1:-1]):
        out.append(block.indent + line + suffix)
    out.append(block.indent + drawn[-1])
    return out


def _substitute(line: str, skin_name: str, values: dict[str, str], ledger_view: str, width: int) -> list[str]:
    """Fill every placeholder on one line; a value with newlines becomes more lines, same indent."""
    keys = _PLACEHOLDER.findall(line)
    if not keys:
        return [line]
    indent = line[: len(line) - len(line.lstrip())]
    filled = line
    for key in keys:
        filled = filled.replace("{{" + key + "}}", _value_for(key, skin_name, values, ledger_view, width))
    pieces = filled.split("\n")
    return [pieces[0]] + [indent + piece for piece in pieces[1:]]


def _value_for(key: str, skin_name: str, values: dict[str, str], ledger_view: str, width: int) -> str:
    """One placeholder's value. The ledger rows are shaped per skin and to the width they land in."""
    if key == LEDGER_ROWS:
        return _ledger_rows(skin_name, ledger_view, width)
    try:
        return values[key]
    except KeyError:
        raise SkinError(f"{skin_name}: unknown placeholder {{{{{key}}}}}") from None


def _placeholder_values(
    decision: Decision,
    skin_name: str,
    activity: str,
    ledger_summary: dict[str, Any] | None,
    next_contract_id: str,
    moment: datetime,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Every placeholder except the ledger rows, which depend on the skin they land in."""
    data = ledger_summary or {}
    title = str(data.get("title") or NO_CONTRACT_TITLE)
    total = _count(data.get("total"))
    built = _count(data.get("built"))
    proven = _count(data.get("proven"))
    return {
        "stamp": f"{moment:%d %b %H:%M}".upper(),
        "clock": f"{moment:%H:%M}",
        "tminus": _tminus(data.get("due"), moment),
        "contract_id": _contract_id(skin_name, data, next_contract_id),
        "title": title,
        "title_upper": title.upper(),
        "built_bar": f"{bar(built, total)} {built}/{total}",
        "proven_bar": f"{bar(proven, total)} {proven}/{total}",
        "proven": str(proven),
        "open": str(_count(data.get("open"))),
        "catches": str(_count(data.get("catches"))),
        "activity": activity or spark([0] * ACTIVITY_BUCKETS),
        "tier": decision.tier,
    }


def _contract_id(skin_name: str, data: dict[str, Any], next_contract_id: str) -> str:
    """The number this turn prints: the next one on a contract turn, the live one everywhere else."""
    if skin_name == FRESH_CONTRACT_SKIN:
        return next_contract_id
    return str(data.get("contract_id") or next_contract_id)


def _count(value: Any) -> int:
    """A ledger count as an integer; anything unreadable counts as none."""
    try:
        return int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0


def _tminus(due: Any, moment: datetime) -> str:
    """The ` · T-minus 2d 19h to Friday` tail, or nothing when there is no readable due date."""
    if not due:
        return ""
    try:
        when = datetime.fromisoformat(str(due))
    except ValueError:
        return ""
    if (when.tzinfo is None) != (moment.tzinfo is None):
        return ""  # why: one aware and one naive cannot be compared, and a guess would be worse
    delta = when - moment
    if delta.total_seconds() <= 0:
        return f" · overdue since {when:%A}"
    return f" · T-minus {delta.days}d {delta.seconds // 3600}h to {when:%A}"


def _parse_ledger(view: str) -> list[_LedgerRow]:
    """Read ``ledger.view()`` back into rows: the id, the status glyph, the text and the proof."""
    rows: list[_LedgerRow] = []
    for line in view.strip("\n").split("\n"):
        match = _LEDGER_ROW.match(line)
        if match is None:
            continue
        rest = match.group(3)
        text, _, proof = rest.partition(_PROOF_SPLIT)
        rows.append(_LedgerRow(rid=match.group(1), glyph=match.group(2) or "", text=text.strip(), proof=proof.strip()))
    return rows


def _ledger_rows(skin_name: str, view: str, width: int) -> str:
    """The ledger, shaped for the skin asking: chips for SITREP, till rows for the receipt."""
    rows = _parse_ledger(view)
    if not rows:
        return NO_ROWS
    if skin_name == "sitrep":
        return _chips(rows, width)
    if skin_name == "receipt":
        return "\n".join(f"{row.rid}  {row.text[:RECEIPT_LABEL_WIDTH].ljust(RECEIPT_LABEL_WIDTH)}{row.glyph}"[:width] for row in rows)
    return "\n".join(line[:width] for line in view.strip("\n").split("\n"))


def _chips(rows: Iterable[_LedgerRow], width: int) -> str:
    """One chip per row (`R1 works on mobile ●`), packed greedily into lines of ``width``."""
    chips = [f"{row.rid} {row.text[:CHIP_LABEL_MAX].rstrip()} {row.glyph}".rstrip() for row in rows]
    lines: list[str] = []
    current = ""
    for chip in chips:
        candidate = chip if not current else current + CHIP_GAP + chip
        if len(candidate) > width and current:
            lines.append(current)
            current = chip
            continue
        current = candidate
    if current:
        lines.append(current)
    return "\n".join(line[:width] for line in lines)



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/sparkline.py
================================================
"""Two tiny drawings: the activity sparkline and the progress bar.

Both are pure functions over counts, so a skin never has to know where the numbers came
from. Every cell is one character wide, so a line's column count is its character count
and the 88-column law can be checked by measuring the string.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

SPARK = "▁▂▃▄▅▆▇█"  # why: eight block heights, lowest first; index 0 is the empty bucket
FULL = "█"  # why: the filled cell of a progress bar, the same block the sparkline tops out at
EMPTY = "░"  # why: the unfilled cell; a lighter shade so the split reads at a glance
BAR_CELLS = 14  # why: the built and proven bars are 14 cells wide in the signed SITREP frame


def spark(counts: Sequence[int]) -> str:
    """One block character per count, scaled linearly to the largest count.

    The result is exactly as long as ``counts``. An empty run of counts draws nothing; a run
    that is all zeros draws the lowest block, so an idle window still shows its shape.
    """
    if not counts:
        return ""
    safe = [count if count > 0 else 0 for count in counts]
    top = max(safe)
    if top == 0:
        return SPARK[0] * len(safe)
    levels = len(SPARK) - 1
    # why: integer round-half-up, so the tallest bucket always lands on the last block
    return "".join(SPARK[(count * levels * 2 + top) // (2 * top)] for count in safe)


def bar(done: int, total: int, cells: int = BAR_CELLS) -> str:
    """A ``cells``-wide progress bar: filled blocks then empty ones, rounded half up.

    ``total`` of zero or less draws an empty bar rather than raising: a contract with no rows
    yet is a normal state, not an error. ``done`` is clamped to ``total`` so a double-counted
    row can never draw past the end.
    """
    if cells <= 0:
        return ""
    if total <= 0:
        return EMPTY * cells
    filled = min(max(done, 0), total)
    lit = (filled * cells * 2 + total) // (2 * total)
    return FULL * lit + EMPTY * (cells - lit)



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/telemetry.py
================================================
"""Every routing decision as one JSON line, and the heartbeat read back out of those lines.

``runs.jsonl`` is the only record of what the router did, so writing it can never cost a turn:
``append`` swallows its own failures and logs them. The sparkline the SITREP skin shows is these
lines counted into buckets, so what is on screen is real data rather than decoration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from lib import hookio, sparkline
from lib.score import Decision

RUNS_NAME = "runs.jsonl"  # why: one line per decision, so two sessions can append at once without clobbering
ROUTE_EVENT = "route"  # why: the router's own event name; the gate and the restore hook write their own
DEFAULT_WINDOW_MINUTES = 120  # why: two hours is one working session, the span the heartbeat covers
DEFAULT_BUCKETS = 20  # why: twenty cells is the sparkline width the frames render
DEFAULT_RECENT = 5  # why: five past turns is what the restore line needs to say what a session was doing


def runs_path() -> Path:
    """Where the telemetry lines go."""
    return hookio.state_dir() / RUNS_NAME


def append(
    decision: Decision,
    session_id: str,
    event: str = ROUTE_EVENT,
    extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Append one line for this decision. Never raises: a telemetry failure costs a line, not the turn."""
    try:
        record: dict[str, Any] = {
            "ts": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "session_id": session_id,
            "event": event,
            "skin": decision.skin,
            "tier": decision.tier,
            "score": float(decision.score),
            "source": decision.source,
            "signals": {name: value for name, value in decision.signals_top3},
        }
        if extra:
            record.update(extra)
        with open(runs_path(), "a", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — the never-crash law: telemetry is never worth a turn
        try:
            hookio.log(f"telemetry: could not append a run ({exc})")
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — logging the log failure would be the same trap again
            return None
    return None


def _records() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Every readable line in the file. A torn or hand-edited line is skipped, not fatal."""
    try:
        raw = runs_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        return []
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            record = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if isinstance(record, dict):
            out.append(record)
    return out


def _parse_ts(value: Any) -> datetime | None:
    """A line's timestamp as a local naive datetime, or ``None`` when it cannot be read."""
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return None
    try:
        stamp = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    if stamp.tzinfo is not None:
        stamp = stamp.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)
    return stamp


def activity(
    window_minutes: int = DEFAULT_WINDOW_MINUTES,
    buckets: int = DEFAULT_BUCKETS,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> str:
    """The routing heartbeat: route events per bucket over the window, drawn as a sparkline.

    An empty or unreadable file gives one ``▁`` per bucket, so a fresh clone shows a flat line
    rather than a gap.
    """
    moment = now if now is not None else datetime.now()
    cells = max(int(buckets), 1)
    counts = [0] * cells
    window = timedelta(minutes=window_minutes)
    start = moment - window
    bucket_seconds = window.total_seconds() / cells
    if bucket_seconds <= 0:
        return sparkline.spark(counts)

    for record in _records():
        if record.get("event") != ROUTE_EVENT:
            continue
        stamp = _parse_ts(record.get("ts"))
        if stamp is None or stamp < start:
            continue
        index = int((stamp - start).total_seconds() / bucket_seconds)
        counts[min(max(index, 0), cells - 1)] += 1
    return sparkline.spark(counts)


def recent(session_id: str, n: int = DEFAULT_RECENT) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """The last ``n`` lines this session wrote, oldest first."""
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    hits = [record for record in _records() if record.get("session_id") == session_id]
    return hits[-n:]



================================================
FILE: .claude/hooks/lib/transcript.py
================================================
"""Read the session transcript the harness points at: the reply to this turn, and recent turns.

The transcript is written by Claude Code, not by us, so nothing here trusts it: a line that does
not parse is skipped, a line that does not decode is skipped, and a path that is missing or
unreadable gives "". A reader that raised would cost the member their turn, and the Stop gate
would lose the reply it is meant to check. The file is read a line at a time and only what is
asked for is held, because a long session's transcript runs to tens of megabytes.

Two content shapes exist in the wire format and both are handled: ``message.content`` as a plain
string, and as a list of blocks where only the ``{"type": "text"}`` ones carry words.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time
from collections import deque
from collections.abc import Iterator
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

USER_ROLE = "user"  # why: the two roles the transcript carries that a reader cares about
ASSISTANT_ROLE = "assistant"
ROLES = (USER_ROLE, ASSISTANT_ROLE)

DEFAULT_TURNS = 4  # why: four turns is enough context for the classifier and small enough to stay cheap
TURN_CLIP_CHARS = 300  # why: a turn is a hint, not a transcript; 300 characters keeps four of them small
TEXT_BLOCK = "text"  # why: the only block type in a content list that holds words

# why: the harness writes the reply a moment after the Stop hook starts, so the file is re-read a
# few times before giving up. Eight reads a quarter of a second apart wait 1.75 seconds in total,
# which covers the flush and stays well inside the 60 seconds Claude Code allows a hook.
REPLY_ATTEMPTS = 8
REPLY_DELAY_SECONDS = 0.25


def last_assistant_text(transcript_path: str | Path | None) -> str:
    """The assistant's reply to the newest prompt, or "" when it is not in the file yet.

    Only an assistant entry that comes after the last user entry counts, because that is the reply
    to the turn being checked. When the newest entry is the member's, the model has not answered
    yet and the honest answer is nothing: the reply above it belongs to the turn before, and the
    Stop gate would otherwise check a contract table the member has already dealt with.

    Line breaks are kept exactly as written: the gate parses a contract table out of this, and a
    flattened reply would have no rows.
    """
    latest = ""
    for entry in _entries(transcript_path):
        role = _role(entry)
        if role == USER_ROLE:
            # why: a new prompt closes the previous reply, tool results included; whatever the
            # model says next is the answer to this one
            latest = ""
            continue
        if role != ASSISTANT_ROLE:
            continue
        text = _content_text(_content(entry)).strip()
        if text:
            latest = text
    return latest


def wait_for_reply(
    transcript_path: str | Path | None,
    attempts: int = REPLY_ATTEMPTS,
    delay_s: float = REPLY_DELAY_SECONDS,
) -> str:
    """The reply to the newest prompt, re-reading the file until it lands or the attempts run out.

    A Stop hook can start before the harness has flushed the reply it is meant to check, and a
    reader that looked once would call that turn empty and let a contract table through unrecorded.
    With no path there is nothing to wait for, so that answers "" straight away.
    """
    if transcript_path is None or not str(transcript_path).strip():
        return ""
    tries = max(int(attempts), 1)
    for attempt in range(tries):
        text = last_assistant_text(transcript_path)
        if text:
            return text
        if attempt + 1 < tries:
            time.sleep(delay_s)
    return ""


def recent_turns(transcript_path: str | Path | None, n: int = DEFAULT_TURNS) -> str:
    """The last ``n`` user and assistant turns, one line each, clipped. "" when unavailable."""
    if n <= 0:
        return ""
    turns: deque[str] = deque(maxlen=n)
    for entry in _entries(transcript_path):
        role = _role(entry)
        if not role:
            continue
        text = " ".join(_content_text(_content(entry)).split())
        if not text:
            continue
        turns.append(f"{role}: {text[:TURN_CLIP_CHARS]}")
    return "\n".join(turns)


def _entries(transcript_path: str | Path | None) -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Every readable JSON object in the file, oldest first. A bad line costs that line only."""
    if transcript_path is None or not str(transcript_path).strip():
        return
    try:
        stream = open(Path(transcript_path), "rb")
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return
    with stream:
        for chunk in stream:
            chunk = chunk.strip()
            if not chunk:
                continue
            try:
                line = chunk.decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                continue
            try:
                entry = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if isinstance(entry, dict):
                yield entry


def _role(entry: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """``user``, ``assistant`` or "" for everything else (summaries, system lines, tool records)."""
    message = entry.get("message")
    role = message.get("role") if isinstance(message, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(role, str):
        role = entry.get("role")
    if not isinstance(role, str):
        role = entry.get("type")
    role = role.lower() if isinstance(role, str) else ""
    return role if role in ROLES else ""


def _content(entry: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    """The content field, whether it sits under ``message`` or at the top level."""
    message = entry.get("message")
    if isinstance(message, dict) and "content" in message:
        return message.get("content")
    return entry.get("content")


def _content_text(content: Any) -> str:
    """Words out of a content field: a plain string, or the text blocks of a list."""
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        parts: list[str] = []
        for block in content:
            if not isinstance(block, dict) or block.get("type") != TEXT_BLOCK:
                continue
            text = block.get("text")
            if isinstance(text, str):
                parts.append(text)
        return "\n".join(parts)
    return ""

================================================
FILE: .claude/output-styles/router.md
================================================
---
name: Router
description: renders the skin the router picked; the hook does the deciding
keep-coding-instructions: true
---

Every reply takes the shape the turn asks for.

When the turn carries a [router] block:

- Render exactly the skin it names, inside one ```text fence. When the block says the skin takes no fence, render it as plain lines with no fence at all.
- Put the header first, copied character for character.
- Keep every fixed word, label and glyph verbatim. Replace each <slot> with your own content for this turn.
- Keep the column alignment. Pad with spaces, never tabs.
- No line wider than 88 columns; wrap slot text under its label rather than past it.
- The fence is the whole reply: nothing before it, nothing after it. Anything you need to say goes in a slot; a skin that allows a line after the fence says so in its notes.
- Never mention the router, the rules, the scores, or this instruction. The skin is the only announcement.
- When the block carries a "tie → model" line, pick the skin that fits the conversation better and render that one on its own.

When the turn carries no [router] block, from a subagent or with hooks switched off, answer normally.

Voice inside every slot: short sentences, plain words, no "it's not X, it's Y".
Never the long dash (—) anywhere in a reply, use a comma, a colon or " · ".
